Feral Druid DPS Pre-Raid Best in Slot (BiS) - TBC Classic
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/01/29
In this Pre-Raid Best-in-Slot Gear List for Feral Druid DPS in The Burning Crusade Classic, we will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid DPS to aim for when gearing up to raids in The Burning Crusade Classic, including gear sourced from Normal and Heroic Dungeons, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.


Feral Druid DPS Best in Slot Hub
Pre-Raid Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid DPS in Phase 1
In this guide, the top recommended items in each slot will be pieces that can be acquired from questing, easily grinded reputation, or normal mode dungeons, even though there are usually better options from heroic dungeons or Badges of Justice. The reason for this is that Karazhan is known to be easier than pre-nerf heroic dungeons, and most players will run through Karazhan before farming heroics. This guide aims to prepare players for their first foray into raiding using easily acquired gear pieces. For subsequent 5-man and PvP gear progression outside of raids, the full ranking of potential options is also provided below the recommended pre-raid piece.

Since several of the gear pieces in this set are temporary and will be quickly replaced with raid gear, we believe it is not worth spending lots of gold on pricy gems, so Uncommon quality gems will work just as well until you replace Pre-Raid with BiS gear. Likewise, budget versions of the shoulder and leg enchants are used since both pieces will be replaced during Phase 1.

Feral Druids looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Gems & Enchants
Should Feral DPS Choose Aldor or Scryer for BiS in TBC Classic Phase 1?
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For Feral DPS, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants: Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance and Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. Despite providing different distributions of Attack Power versus Critical Strike Rating, simulations show that both enchants provide identical DPS value to within the simulation resolution of +/- 1 DPS. So feel free to choose either faction based on personal preference, as the choice will not impact your DPS output whatsoever in the long run.

Overall, by a very minute edge, we'd recommend the Aldor for Feral Druid Tanks.

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Pre-Raid Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid in TBC Classic Phase 1
Wolfshead Helm is the only viable helm option for cats, both in vanilla and throughout TBC. Powershifting is such a massive DPS increase from a conventional energy-limited rotation that no DPS stats come remotely close to matching the benefit of this helm, even with the significantly better stats on TBC gear pieces.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm	Profession: Leatherworking (BoE)
Pre-Raid Best in Slot Shoulder Armor for Feral Druid in TBC Classic Phase 1
Wastewalker Shoulderpads is the overall best shoulder armor for Pre Raid, offering gem sockets, high Agility, Attack Power, and Hit Rating
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Wastewalker Shoulderpads Wastewalker Shoulderpads	Drop: Avatar of the Martyred (Heroic Auchenai Crypts)
Alliance Alternative	Expedition Scout's Epaulets Expedition Scout's Epaulets	Quest:Fel Embers Fel Embers
Alternative	Bladed Shoulderpads of the Merciless Bladed Shoulderpads of the Merciless	Drop: Echo of Medivh (Karazhan Chess Event)
Alternative	Mantle of Perenolde Mantle of Perenolde	Drop: Epoch Hunter (Old Hillsbrad Foothills)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Back Armor for Feral Druid in TBC Classic Phase 1
Cloak of the Craft is the best cloak you can get prior to entering Karazhan, but it is a world drop and may be in short supply. Buying this cloak is a good investment, however, because it is only replaced by a single Karazhan cloak, contested by all physical DPS players.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Cloak of the Craft Cloak of the Craft	World Drop: The Outland

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Chest Armor for Feral Druid in TBC Classic Phase 1
Tunic of Assassination is the recommended pre-raid chest piece to get prior to raids and heroics. The absolute best pre-raid chest piece is technically Primalstrike Vest, but it is strongly recommended that you not invest your gold in crafting this. The mats for the Primalstrike set are extremely expensive, and they do not last long enough to justify the investment, being quickly replaced by raid drops.

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Neck Jewelry for Feral Druid in TBC Classic Phase 1
Braided Eternium Chain is the highest DPS neckpiece for cats in Phase 1 when using its on-use buff. Weapon damage is also a particularly strong DPS stat for feral druids due to their fast 1.0-second attack speed. Moreover, once you account for the 3-4 additional party members who will also benefit from the bonus crit rating, the total raid DPS value of this neckpiece is miles above any alternatives.

When raiding on a budget, a more permanent neck option is Necklace of Trophies, which is your best early pickup before raids or heroics.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Braided Eternium Chain Braided Eternium Chain	Braided Eternium Chain - Jewelcrafting (360)
Best	Choker of Vile Intent Choker of Vile Intent	Vendor: G'eras (25 Badges of Justice)
Alternative	Necklace of Trophies Necklace of Trophies	World Drop: The Outland
Alternative	Haramad's Bargain Haramad's Bargain	Vendor: Paulsta'ats / Karaaz - Requires Exalted with The Consortium

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Ring Jewelry for Feral Druid in TBC Classic Phase 1
While not individually the highest DPS ring available, Shapeshifter's Signet is nonetheless the most valuable ring because it loses minimal DPS compared to alternatives while shifting hit rating to expertise rating, which is far harder to cap. In raids without a Balance Druid, you'll want to pair this with Shaffar's Band of Brutality

Overseer's Signet is the best DPS ring outside of raids and Heroics, but is restricted only to those who picked the Aldor faction.
Rank	Item	Source

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid in TBC Classic Phase 1
Just as in vanilla, Mark of the Champion is a best-in-slot trinket by a large margin for all TBC undead/demon encounters, which encompasses about half of the bosses in Karazhan as well as Magtheridon. For bosses that are neither undead nor demon, Bloodlust Brooch and Hourglass of the Unraveller are the top two options, respectively, outside of raiding.

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
The only competitive weapons for feral druids throughout the entirety of TBC are two-handed staves and maces, so one-handed options need not be considered at all. For early pre-raid gear progression, Staff of Natural Fury is recommended as it can be purchased from the auction house and outperforms all other easily acquired weapons; however, it will more than likely be an expensive purchase, as it is a random BoE world drop in the Outland. Your non-BoE best will be Feral Staff of Lashing.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Staff of Natural Fury Staff of Natural Fury	World Drop: The Outland
Best	Feral Staff of Lashing Feral Staff of Lashing	Drop: Warp Splinter (Heroic The Botanica)
Alternative	Earthwarden Earthwarden	Vendor: Fedryen Swiftspear - Requires Exalted with Cenarion Expedition
Alternative	Draenic Wildstaff Draenic Wildstaff	Drop: Avatar of the Martyred (Heroic Auchenai Crypts)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Weapon Recommendations

You shouldn't have much competition with weapons, as your only other competition will be other Feral Druids. However, these listed below are good placeholders until you obtain your best options.

Alternative	Dreamer's Dragonstaff Dreamer's Dragonstaff	Drop: Thorngrin the Tender (The Botanica)

Alternative	Grand Marshal's Maul Grand Marshal's Maul / High Warlord's Maul High Warlord's Maul	36000 Honor Points, 40 Alterac Valley Mark of Honor Alterac Valley Mark of Honor - Alterac Valley

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Idols for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
Your very first priority when farming Badges from heroic dungeons should be acquiring Everbloom Idol, as it is a hefty upgrade over the pre-raid options and will last you through multiple content phases.
Rank	Item	Source

NerdEgghead
Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

By heijgun (12,782 – 2·10·20) on 2021/05/24 (Burning Crusade)	
	
Will Wolfshead still be BiS with the energy tick changes?

3

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/05/25 (Burning Crusade)
Yes, absolutely. Powershifting is required no matter what version of energy ticks is used, it massively increases the number of special abilities you can use in a fight. The only thing that the new ticks do is to make the powershift cycle slower than it used to be. In fact, Wolfshead is even *more* mandatory under the new tick system: the tick reset means that we have less energy leeway per cycle than before, so the extra 20 energy from Wolfshead is absolutely critical for the rotation to even work.

Shattrath Leggings Shattrath Leggings - Reward from the chain to get the Arcatraz key.

Here's what I had, admittedly I've got to revise it anyway.
https://www.wowhead.com/tbc/gear-planner/druid/tauren/A0YQ9QMDITIyIQUzElHwVQMAH4EAIJmKfAIAcsWDQGyVim8AXdwgXeGFYG5obTgAXf0gXf1AXf0GAG0HhwB2MYqgiABs24TXiQB5x2z7igB2hWH4CwB4cgwAaz0OAG2CjwBs9ITUkAB5IW1JEgBu1A

Last edited by Enax on 2021/05/17

2

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/05/18 (Burning Crusade)
There is no reason to target any particular value of Expertise or try and stack it on your gear. Expertise Rating is worth exactly the same value as Hit Rating for DPS. There is no need whatsoever to try and cap these stats. Hit and Expertise simply have a DPS value that you can compare to the value of 1 Attack Power. Valuing these stats more than their AP equivalent will lower your DPS, so it can easily be the case that the highest DPS gear set is not Hit or Expertise capped. The cap is simply something you cannot exceed, and just limits the value of the stat.

-1

	
By thux2828 (1,539 – 7·5) on 2021/06/16 (Burning Crusade)	
	
the 20 extra energy from Wolfshead results in 80 energy per powershift
How come 80 energy? There is 40 from Furor and 20 from Wolfshead helm. How do we get the other 20?
Last edited by thux2828 on 2021/06/16

-1

	

ferugalkurn on 2021/06/21 (Burning Crusade)
when i looked it up elsewhere it said 60 so i think the 80 is possibly just a mistake

3

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/06/21 (Burning Crusade)
My wording there was a bit ambiguous, my apologies. The combination of Furor and Wolfshead gives you 60 Energy immediately upon a shift as you stated, but the next Energy tick bumps that up to 80. Without Wolfshead, you would only get 60 Energy after the tick, which is not enough for the powershift cycle to work.

Feral Druid DPS Phase 1 Best in Slot (BiS) - TBC Classic
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/02/01
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Karazhan, Gruul's Lair, and Magtheridon's Lair for Feral Druid DPS in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid DPS to acquire while they progress on the first few raids in The Burning Crusade Classic, and contains gear sourced from Karazhan, Gruul's Lair, Magtheridon's Lair, heroic dungeons, early PvP grinding, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.

We have created many different best-in-slot lists, including a pre-raid list, containing gear you can obtain without doing any raid content.


Feral Druid DPS Best in Slot Guides
Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid DPS in Phase 1
In this guide, recommendations in each slot are given in three different ways: the best piece individually in that slot, the optimal piece to use in a 9% hit raid set (hard hit cap), and the optimal piece to use in a 5-6% hit raid set, where you would have a Balance Druid providing the Improved Faerie Fire debuff and have a Draenei Draenei in your group to provide their racial buff. Heroic Presence. These three categories are not always the same, as Phase 1 melee gear is generally overloaded with hit rating, so simply optimizing gear for individual DPS value leads to sets that far exceed the hit cap. For this reason, given two comparable pieces in a gear slot, the option with less hit on it is preferred as it frees up "hit budget" that you can use in other slots.

Feral Druids looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Gems & Enchants
Should Feral DPS Choose Aldor or Scryer for BiS in TBC Classic Phase 1?
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For Feral DPS, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants: Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance and Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. However, these items provide an effectively identical increase, so simply choose whichever you'd prefer.

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
Wolfshead Helm is the only viable helm option for cats, both in vanilla and throughout TBC. Powershifting is such a massive DPS increase from a conventional energy-limited rotation that no DPS stats come remotely close to matching the benefit of this helm, even with the significantly better stats on TBC gear pieces.

Rank	Item	Source
Best	Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm	Profession: Leatherworking (BoE)
Best in Slot Shoulder Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
Mantle of Malorne is the best shoulder piece for feral druids in Phase 1, and should be relatively easy to acquire as a tier piece.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Mantle of Malorne Mantle of Malorne	Drop: Tier Token from High King Maulgar (Gruul's Lair)
Close Second	Wastewalker Shoulderpads Wastewalker Shoulderpads	Drop: Avatar of the Martyred (Heroic Auchenai Crypts)

Best in Slot Chest Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
Breastplate of Malorne is the best chest piece to use in any Phase 1 gear set, both for tanking and for DPS. Individually Terrorweave Tunic provides higher DPS, but when combined with the Tier 4 shoulders, the 2-piece energy regeneration set bonus causes the Tier 4 chest to win out.

Best in Slot Wrist Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
Nightfall Wristguards are the recommended choice in the wrist slot. The absolute top two best in slot options are the of the Tiger and of Agility variants of Ravager's Wrist-Wraps, respectively. However, there are three levels of RNG for these items to drop: 1. The right boss of the three possible choices needs to spawn, 2. The right item needs to drop, and 3. the right variant needs to be added to said item (there are twenty-eight variants). So, just stick with the first option.

Best in Slot Neck Jewelry for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
Braided Eternium Chain is the highest DPS neckpiece for cats in Phase 1 when using its on-use buff. Weapon damage is also a particularly strong DPS stat for feral druids due to their fast 1.0-second attack speed. Moreover, once you account for the 3-4 additional party members who will also benefit from the bonus crit rating, the total raid DPS value of this neckpiece is miles above any alternatives.

When raiding on a budget, a more permanent neck option is Choker of Vile Intent, which is the second-best neck piece in Phase 1 and fortunately does not rely on any random raid drops.

Best in Slot Ring Jewelry for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
While not individually the highest DPS ring available, Shapeshifter's Signet is nonetheless the most valuable ring because it loses minimal DPS compared to alternatives while shifting hit rating to expertise rating, which is far harder to cap. In raids without a Balance Druid, Garona's Signet Ring is the optimal ring to pair with the former, packing the most individual DPS in the slot. If Improved Faerie Fire is used, then Ring of the Recalcitrant is the optimal pairing to use instead. Note that picking up this ring prevents you from getting the tanking variant, so consider your choice carefully. If you tank more than occasionally in your raid, the tanking ring is recommended as it has no real alternatives until Sunwell, whereas there are plenty of good DPS rings in subsequent raid tiers.

Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
Just as in vanilla, Mark of the Champion is a best-in-slot trinket by a large margin for all TBC Undead/Demon encounters in Phase 1, which encompasses about half of the bosses in Karazhan as well as Magtheridon. For bosses that are neither undead nor demon, Dragonspine Trophy is the clear winner and can last for many phases, but is highly contested by all physical DPS classes. The next best trinket option is Bloodlust Brooch, which is the second most important Badge of Justice Badge of Justice reward for feral druids to prioritize after obtaining our BiS Idol.

Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 1
The only competitive weapons for Feral Druids throughout the entirety of TBC are two-handed staves and maces, so one-handed options need not be considered at all.

The weapon slot is another example of the best in slot gear choice changing for raids with Balance Druids, since not all of the hit rating on Terestian's Stranglestaff can be utilized in that scenario. In addition, it is worth noting that even though the pre-raid weapon Staff of Natural Fury is replaced for raw DPS, this staff is worth keeping around as it remains situationally useful for long encounters where you cannot sustain a permanent powershifting rotation without the reduced mana cost.
Rank	Item	Source

Alternative	Feral Staff of Lashing Feral Staff of Lashing	Drop: Warp Splinter (Heroic The Botanica)
Alternative	Staff of Natural Fury Staff of Natural Fury	World Drop: The Outland

You shouldn't have much competition with weapons, as your only other competition will be other Feral Druids. However, the ones listed below are good placeholders until you obtain your best options.

Your very first priority when farming Badges from heroic dungeons should be acquiring Everbloom Idol, as it is a hefty upgrade over the pre-raid options and will last you through multiple content phases. You shouldn't spend too much time without your best idol, but the ones below will be solid placeholders until you gather up 15 Badges of Justice to get your best option.

Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

By Oromasdes (965 – 1·6) on 2021/06/14 (Burning Crusade)	
	
Is the Wolfshead and Power Shifting section still accurate with the change to how Energy ticks work?

Even if you shift just before a tick, the games puts you back down to a flat 60, resetting the tick after you've changed.

Does that still work out to being more powerful than top tier stuff?

1

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/06/21 (Burning Crusade)
Yes, even with the Energy changes, powershifting with Wolfshead Helm is an enormous DPS increase. Even the best endgame helms from Sunwell will not compete with the DPS increase from Wolfshead. In fact, the new Energy changes make Wolfshead even *more* essential because the new rotations have much less Energy leeway than before.

4

	

WallHackJack on 2021/09/15 (Burning Crusade)
For future readers, this is in reference to TBC launching with scuffed energy regen, it's been reverted back to normal

2

	
By Enkidiev (584 – 3·4) on 2021/07/01 (Burning Crusade)	
	
I still have a big question mark hanging around, how come "on hit effect" trinket like DST or Hourglass ar recommended as best trinket when to my knowledge, druids can't proc on-hits? (I mean if it were we'd be using monsooge instead of Agi on staves right?)

Quite confused, if someone has the answer i'd be glad ♥.
Last edited by Enkidiev on 2021/07/01

1

	

LearnToSwim on 2021/07/02 (Burning Crusade)
Things like Mongoose/Windfury are specifically on weapon hit, not just a melee hit, which means Ferals won't proc it because we don't hit with weapons. While "on-hit" generally means a melee hit, which applies to things like DST or Hourglass.

1

	

Mickypicky on 2021/07/03 (Burning Crusade)
Anything that says "Equip: Chance on hit" works in forms. Anything that only says "Chance on hit" does NOT work in forms.

0

	

Enkidiev on 2021/07/05 (Burning Crusade)
Ok , I get the difference =). Good to know, thanks a lot!

By OsirisII (4,588 – 11·10) on 2021/08/22 (Burning Crusade)	
	
Why aren't the Primalstrike Bracers considered as optional?

Nightfall Wristguards
153 Armor
+24 Agility
+22 Stamina
Equip: Increases attack power by 46
Vs
Primalstrike Bracers
159 Armor
+15 Agility
+21 Stamina
Equip: Increases attack power by 64.

3

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/08/24 (Burning Crusade)
1 Agility is worth around 3 AP equivalent for DPS, so Primalstrike are worse than Nightfall by about 9 AP equivalent. This also puts them very slightly lower than Qiraji Execution Bracers in value, and a fair bit lower than Spymistress. They are a fine option, but not recommended by any means due to their high cost.

Feral Druid DPS Best in Slot (BiS) Phase 2 Gear Guide - TBC Classic
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/05/12
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Tempest Keep and Serpentshrine Cavern for Feral Druid DPS in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid DPS to acquire while they progress during the second phase of The Burning Crusade Classic, and contains gear sourced from Tempest Keep and Serpentshrine Cavern, plus other pieces from previous phases like Karazhan, Gruul's Lair, Magtheridon's Lair, heroic dungeons, early PvP grinding, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.

We have created many different best in slot lists, including a TBCC pre-patch list and a pre-raid list, containing gear you can obtain without doing any raid content.


Feral Druid DPS Best in Slot HubFeral Druid DPS Pre-Raid Best in Slot

Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid DPS in Phase 2
In this guide, recommendations in each slot are given in three different ways: the best piece individually in that slot, the optimal piece to use in a 9% hit raid set (hard hit cap), and the optimal piece to use in a 5-6% hit raid set (soft cap with Improved Faerie Fire and possibly Draenei hit buff). These three categories are not always the same, as Phase 2 melee gear is generally overloaded with hit rating, so simply optimizing gear for individual DPS value leads to sets that far exceed the hit cap. For this reason, given two comparable pieces in a gear slot, the option with less hit on it is preferred as it frees up "hit budget" that you can use in other slots.

There are actually two different gearing approaches in Phase 2 that exhibit similar performance, but are optimized for different DPS rotations. The absolute top DPS possible is achieved using a combination of the 2-piece Tier 4 set bonus with the best DPS off-pieces from Phase 2. This gear set is optimal when weaving in both  Rip and  Ferocious Bite as finishing moves at 5 Combo Points, prioritizing Rip when the debuff has completely fallen off and Bite otherwise.

Feral Druids looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Gems & Enchants
Should Feral Druid DPS Choose Aldor or Scryer for BiS in TBC Classic Phase 2?
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For Feral DPS, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants: Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance and Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. However, these items provide an effectively identical increase, so simply choose whichever you'd prefer.

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 2
Wolfshead Helm is the only viable helm option for cats, both in vanilla and throughout TBC. Powershifting is such a massive DPS increase from a conventional energy-limited rotation that no DPS stats come remotely close to matching the benefit of this helm, even with the significantly better stats on TBC gear pieces. Specifically, compared to just the baseline Furor talent, the 20 extra energy from Wolfshead results in 80 energy per powershift after the next energy tick, which is enough to enable two specials in a four to six-second DPS cycle.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS	Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm	Profession: Leatherworking - can be purchased on the Auction House
Best in Slot Shoulder Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 2
Shoulderpads of the Stranger are individually the best shoulder piece for Feral Druids in Phase 2. However, due to the strong 2-piece and 4-piece bonuses of the Malorne Harness and Nordrassil Harness sets, respectively, either Mantle of Malorne or Nordrassil Feral-Mantle is the optimal choice in the 2pT4 and 4pT5 gear sets described at the start of this guide.
Rank	Item	Source

Best in Slot Neck Jewelry for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 2
When activated, Braided Eternium Chain is the highest DPS neckpiece for cats in Phase 2. Weapon damage is a particularly strong DPS stat for Feral Druids due to their fast 1.0 second attack speed. Moreover, once you account for the 3-4 additional party members who will also benefit from the bonus crit rating, the total raid DPS value of this neckpiece is miles above any alternatives.

When Braided Eternium Chain is on cooldown, Telonicus's Pendant of Mayhem is the highest DPS alternative neckpiece for cats.

Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 2
The absolute best trinket for Feral DPS in Phase 2 is the highly coveted Tsunami Talisman, which beats the next best option by a whopping ~44 AP equivalent! The next three trinkets are more or less equivalent, Bloodlust Brooch being the recommended choice in the second trinket slot, as it is an activated trinket whose on-use can be optimized for the best burst window in an encounter.

If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Trinket Recommendations

The two other alternatives that are listed, Drake Fang Talisman and Icon of Unyielding Courage, actually provide phenomenal DPS value, but are impossible to fit into any optimized gear set without exceeding the hit cap.

Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 2
The only competitive weapons for Feral Druids throughout the entirety of TBC are two-handed staves and maces, so one-handed options need not be considered at all.

The Season 2 Arena weapon is miles above all alternatives in DPS value. There are no new PvE weapons introduced in Phase 2 for cat DPS, only Wildfury Greatstaff for bears. As a result, the next best option outside of PvP is still Terestian's Stranglestaff from Karazhan, which is more than 130 AP equivalent worse than Merciless Gladiator's Maul.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS	Merciless Gladiator's Maul Merciless Gladiator's Maul	Vendor: Arena Points (Season 2)
Option	Terestian's Stranglestaff Terestian's Stranglestaff	Drop: Terestian Illhoof (Karazhan)
Option	Gladiator's Maul Gladiator's Maul	Vendor: Arena Points (Season 1)

Option	Draenic Wildstaff Draenic Wildstaff	Drop: Avatar of the Martyred (Heroic Auchenai Crypts)

Best in Slot Idols for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 2
For personal DPS, Everbloom Idol is a clear winner in the Idol slot, and is a hefty upgrade over the pre-raid options that will last you through multiple content phases. However, Idol of the Raven Goddess will also be available as a quest reward from the Swift Flight Form quest chain.

Idol of the Raven Goddess is a significant personal DPS loss relative to Everbloom Idol, but provides a lot of raid DPS by buffing your entire party. In a raid environment, you should use Idol of the Raven Goddess when placed in a stacked physical DPS group that scales well with Crit Rating, and use Everbloom Idol otherwise.
Rank	Item	Source
Best for Personal DPS	Everbloom Idol Everbloom Idol	Vendor: G'eras (Badges of Justice)
Best for Raid DPS	Idol of the Raven Goddess Idol of the Raven Goddess	Quest: Vanquish the Raven God Vanquish the Raven God (Heroic Sethekk Halls)
Recommended Pre-raid	Idol of Feral Shadows Idol of Feral Shadows	Drop: Zereketh the Unbound (The Arcatraz)
Pre-raid Option	Idol of the Wild Idol of the Wild	Quest: Colossal Menace Colossal Menace
NerdEgghead
Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

would it be wise to break up the 2 set bonus from Malorne set? extra 20 energy usually every 20 sec or so?

0

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/10/15 (Burning Crusade)
Previously, we believed that the 4 set bonus from the Nordrassil set was strong enough relative to the 2pT4 bonus to justify dropping it in Phase 2. However, we recently updated the cat sim to include the possibility of weaving in Ferocious Bite into the standard Rip rotation, and this boosted the value of the 2pT4 proc significantly when wearing BiS Phase 2 off-pieces. Going for either 2pT4 or 4pT5 is totally fine, as both sets sim fairly close. I would personally recommend 4pT5 if you are not very skilled at powershifting, since a lot of the DPS value of the 2pT4 bonus is lost if you don't utilize the procs efficiently while powershifting. The guide has been updated to show optimized gear sets for both configurations at the top.

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/12/12 (Burning Crusade)
No, this is incorrect. Even with 0 points in Feral Aggression, it is higher DPS to wear 2pT4 + BiS off-pieces than to wear 4pT5, provided you weave in both Ferocious Bite and Rip as finishers. However, the difference between the two gear configurations is fairly small, so wearing 4pT5 is perfectly acceptable.

Burning Crusade Classic Feral Druid DPS Best in Slot (BiS) Phase 3 Gear Guide
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/07/31
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Phase 3 Black Temple and Mount Hyjal for Feral Druid DPS in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid DPS to acquire while they progress during the third phase of The Burning Crusade Classic, and contains gear sourced from Black Temple and Mount Hyjal, plus other pieces from previous phases like Serpentshrine Cavern, Tempest Keep, heroic dungeons, arena PvP Gear, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.

Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid DPS in Phase 3
In this guide, recommendations in each slot are given in three different ways: the best piece individually in that slot, the optimal piece to use in a 9% hit raid set (hard hit cap), and the optimal piece to use in a 5-6% hit raid set (soft cap with Improved Faerie Fire and possibly Heroic Presence). These three categories are not always the same, as Phase 3 melee gear is generally overloaded with hit rating, so simply optimizing gear for individual DPS value leads to sets that far exceed the hit cap. For this reason, given two comparable pieces in a gear slot, the option with less hit on it is preferred as it frees up "hit budget" that you can use in other slots.

Feral Druids looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Gems & Enchants



Wolfshead Helm
Choker of Endless Nightmares
Thunderheart Pauldrons
Thalassian Wildercloak
Thunderheart Chestguard
Vindicator's Dragonhide Bracers
Thunderheart Gauntlets
Belt of One-Hundred Deaths
Thunderheart Leggings
Shadowmaster's Boots
Band of the Eternal Champion
Unstoppable Aggressor's Ring
Tsunami Talisman
Bloodlust Brooch
Vengeful Gladiator's Staff
Off Hand
Idol of the Raven Goddess
Link
Level70
Should Feral Druid DPS Choose Aldor or Scryer for BiS in TBC Classic Phase 3?
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For Feral DPS, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants: Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance and Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. However, these items provide an effectively identical increase, so simply choose whichever you'd prefer.

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 3
Wolfshead Helm remains the best helm option for cat DPS in Phase 3, as the higher Shred cast frequency it enables scales with all of the other stat upgrades from Phase 3 gear.

However, Phase 3 marks the first time in Classic WoW that alternative helm options besides Wolfshead can be considered without severely compromising your DPS output. This is because the 2-piece bonus of Thunderheart Harness opens up a new powershifting rotation in Cat Form that can work even without the extra 20 Energy per shift from Wolfshead. Specifically, when DPSing with 2-piece Tier 6 without Wolfshead, you can simply replace the second Shred cast in a standard powershift cycle with a 35 Energy Mangle (Cat) cast instead, resulting in the same 4 second cycle time at the expense of reduced damage dealt.

Rank	Item	Source
BiS	Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm	Profession: Leatherworking - can be purchased on the Auction House
Option (with 2pT6)	Cursed Vision of Sargeras Cursed Vision of Sargeras	Drop: Illidan Stormrage (Black Temple)
Option (with 2pT6)	Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm	Vendor: Arena Points (Season 3)
Best in Slot Shoulder Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 3
Thunderheart Pauldrons are individually the highest DPS shoulder piece for cats in Phase 3, and are also best in slot when tanking in Bear Form. However, until you obtain three other pieces of the Thunderheart Harness set and can unlock the 4-piece bonus, it is optimal to keep wearing your Tier 4 shoulders instead in order to maintain the 2-piece Malorne Harness set bonus.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS (with 4pT6)	Thunderheart Pauldrons Thunderheart Pauldrons	Drop: Mother Shahraz (Black Temple)
Option (with 2pT4)	Mantle of Malorne Mantle of Malorne	Drop: High King Maulgar (Gruul's Lair)
Option (with 4pT5)	Nordrassil Feral-Mantle Nordrassil Feral-Mantle	Drop: Void Reaver (Tempest Keep: The Eye)
Option	Shoulderpads of the Stranger Shoulderpads of the Stranger	Drop: Hydross the Unstable (Serpentshrine Cavern)

If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Shoulder Armor Recommendations

Rank	Item	Source
Option	Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Spaulders Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Spaulders	Vendor: Arena Points (Season 3)

Best in Slot Back Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 3
Shadowmoon Destroyer's Drape is individually the best in slot cloak for Phase 3. However, gear sets using this cloak will eventually exceed the hit cap, so Thalassian Wildercloak is the optimal choice in a fully optimized gear set.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS Individually	Shadowmoon Destroyer's Drape Shadowmoon Destroyer's Drape	Drop: Teron Gorefiend (Black Temple)
BiS when Hit Capped	Thalassian Wildercloak Thalassian Wildercloak	Drop: Kael'thas Sunstrider (Tempest Keep: The Eye)
Option	Drape of the Dark Reavers Drape of the Dark Reavers	Drop: Shade of Aran (Karazhan)

Best in Slot Ring Jewelry for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 3

As a result, a more practical ring pairing for Ferals in Phase 3 is Ring of Lethality and Band of the Eternal Champion once the 9% hit cap is taken into account. However, when raiding with a Balance Druid providing Improved Faerie Fire, even Ring of Lethality cannot be accommodated in your gear set whenever it is your turn to equip Braided Eternium Chain Braided Eternium Chain. In this scenario, the best alternative option is Unstoppable Aggressor's Ring, followed closely by Shapeshifter's Signet.

Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 3
Tsunami Talisman continues to be the absolute best trinket for Feral DPS in Phase 3, beating all alternative options by a substantial margin. The second trinket slot, on the other hand, is a lot more variable and depends on your remaining itemization and on the raid encounter. If you are raiding without the Improved Faerie Fire debuff or the Heroic Presence buff, then Madness of the Betrayer will provide the highest DPS on any Phase 3 encounter.

In contrast, if you are benefiting from party or raid hit buffs, then you will not have enough hit budget available in your gear set to efficiently utilize the hit rating on Madness of the Betrayer. In this scenario, one of three trinkets can be optimal in the second slot: Mark of the Champion is BiS when facing Undead or Demon type mobs (which encompass over half of the raid bosses in Phase 3), Bloodlust Brooch / Empty Mug of Direbrew is BiS against other enemy types when wearing Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm in a pure DPS set, and Dragonspine Trophy is BiS against other enemy types when wearing any other helm in an off-tank set.
Rank	Item	Source

BiS in 9% Hit Set	Madness of the Betrayer Madness of the Betrayer	Drop: The Illidari Council (Black Temple)
BiS in 6% Hit Set (against Undead / Demons)	Mark of the Champion Mark of the Champion	Quest: The Fall of Kel'Thuzad The Fall of Kel'Thuzad (Classic - Naxxramas)
BiS in 6% Hit Set (with Wolfshead)	Bloodlust Brooch Bloodlust Brooch	Vendor: G'eras (Badges of Justice)

Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 3
The only competitive weapons for Feral Druids throughout the entirety of TBC are two-handed staves and maces, so one-handed options need not be considered at all.

The Season 3 Arena weapon, Vengeful Gladiator's Staff, is miles above all alternatives in DPS value. There are no new PvE weapons introduced in Phase 3 for cat DPS, only Pillar of Ferocity for bears. As a result, the next best option is also from arena, just from the previous season, Merciless Gladiator's Maul.

Best in Slot Idols for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 3
For personal DPS, Everbloom Idol remains a clear winner in the Idol slot, and is a hefty upgrade over the pre-raid options that will last you through multiple content phases. Note that when optimizing for your raid's overall performance rather than just your personal DPS parse, you should use Idol of the Raven Goddess instead of Everbloom whenever you are placed in a stacked physical DPS group that scales well with Crit Rating.

Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

Burning Crusade Classic Feral Druid DPS Best in Slot (BiS) Phase 4 Gear Guide
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/02/12
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Phase 4 Zul'Aman for Feral Druid DPS in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid DPS to acquire while they progress during the fourth phase of The Burning Crusade Classic, and contains gear sourced from Zul'Aman, Black Temple, and Mount Hyjal, plus other pieces from previous phases like Serpentshrine Cavern, Tempest Keep, heroic dungeons, arena PvP Gear, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.

Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid DPS in Phase 4
In this guide, recommendations in each slot are given in three different ways: the best piece individually in that slot, the optimal piece to use in a 9% hit raid set (hard hit cap), and the optimal piece to use in a 5-6% hit raid set (soft cap with Improved Faerie Fire and possibly Heroic Presence). These three categories are not always the same, as Phases 3 and 4 melee gear is generally overloaded with hit rating, so simply optimizing gear for individual DPS value leads to sets that far exceed the hit cap. For this reason, given two comparable pieces in a gear slot, the option with less hit on it is preferred as it frees up "hit budget" that you can use in other slots.

Feral Druids looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Gems & Enchants
Should Feral Druid DPS Choose Aldor or Scryer for BiS in TBC Classic Phase 4?
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryers faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For Feral DPS, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants: Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance and Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. Despite providing different distributions of attack Power versus critical strike rating, simulations show that both enchants provide identical DPS value to within the simulation resolution of +/- 1 DPS. So feel free to choose either faction based on personal preference, as the choice will not impact your DPS output whatsoever in the long run. Technically, these enchants are not even used in a true BiS gear set since Might of the Scourge Might of the Scourge is superior to both, but the vast majority of players will not have Sapphiron enchants saved up from Phase 6 of Classic.

There are, however, a few pre-raid items worth pointing out that are exclusive to the two factions. Feral Druids who choose the Aldor faction will have access to Overseer's Signet Overseer's Signet, which is the best available DPS ring outside of raids. The Aldor faction also provides another exclusive ring Kaylaan's Signet Kaylaan's Signet that is only very slightly worse. However, the quest reward Slayer's Mark of the Redemption Slayer's Mark of the Redemption has identical stats to Kaylaan's Signet and is independent of faction choice.

Feral Druids who choose the Scryers faction will have access to Socrethar's Girdle Socrethar's Girdle, which is quite a bit better than the best pre-raid alternative available to both factions, Naaru Belt of Precision Naaru Belt of Precision. Pre-raid belts will be quickly replaced, however, so once again the gear advantage in this slot is very temporary.

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 4
Wolfshead Helm remains the best helm option for cat DPS in Phase 4, as the higher Shred cast frequency it enables scales with all of the other stat upgrades from Phase 4 gear.

However, Phase 3 marked the first time in Classic WoW that alternative helm options besides Wolfshead can be considered without severely compromising your DPS output. This is because the 2-piece bonus of Thunderheart Harness opens up a new powershifting rotation in Cat Form that can work even without the extra 20 Energy per shift from Wolfshead. Specifically, when DPSing with 2-piece Tier 6 without Wolfshead, you can simply replace the second Shred cast in a standard powershift cycle with a 35 Energy Mangle (Cat) cast instead, resulting in the same 4-second cycle time at the expense of reduced damage dealt:
Standard DPS Cycle with Wolfshead Helm	DPS Cycle without Wolfshead Helm using 2-piece Thunderheart Harness bonus
Time in seconds	Action/Event	Final Energy
0	Powershift	60
1	Energy Tick	80
1.5	GCD ends, cast Shred	38
2.5	GCD ends, wait	38
3	Energy Tick	58
3	Cast Shred	16
4	GCD ends, powershift	60
	
Time in seconds	Action/Event	Final Energy
0	Powershift	40
1	Energy Tick	60
1.5	GCD ends, cast Shred	18
2.5	GCD ends, wait	18
3	Energy Tick	38
3	Cast Mangle	3
4	GCD ends, powershift	40
Note that Mangle deals significantly less damage than Shred, which is why Wolfshead Helm still comes out on top as the best helm for most scenarios. However, this damage difference is partially compensated by the strong DPS stats and the meta gem socket on the two best alternative helm options, Cursed Vision of Sargeras and Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm, resulting in only a 10-40 DPS loss from dropping Wolfshead in Phase 4, compared to hundreds of DPS in prior phases. Alternative helms to Wolfshead should really only be considered by Feral off-tanks, however, as the massive mitigation gain from putting on the Season 3 Arena helm while tanking can easily be worth the DPS loss in Cat Form once your tank target is dead.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS	Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm	Profession: Leatherworking - can be purchased on the Auction House
Option (with 2pT6)	Cursed Vision of Sargeras Cursed Vision of Sargeras	Drop: Illidan Stormrage (Black Temple)
Option (with 2pT6)	Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm	Vendor: Arena Points (Season 3)
Best in Slot Shoulder Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 4
Thunderheart Pauldrons are individually the highest DPS shoulder piece for cats in Phase 4, and are also best in slot when tanking in Bear Form. However, until you obtain three other pieces of the Thunderheart Harness set and can unlock the 4-piece bonus, it is optimal to keep wearing your Tier 4 shoulders instead in order to maintain the 2-piece Malorne Harness set bonus, which continues to scale well throughout Phase 4.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS (with 4pT6)	Thunderheart Pauldrons Thunderheart Pauldrons	Drop: Mother Shahraz (Black Temple)
Option (with 2pT4)	Mantle of Malorne Mantle of Malorne	Drop: High King Maulgar (Gruul's Lair)
Option (with 4pT5)	Nordrassil Feral-Mantle Nordrassil Feral-Mantle	Drop: Void Reaver (Tempest Keep: The Eye)
Option	Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Spaulders Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Spaulders	Vendor: Arena Points (Season 3)

If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Shoulder Armor Recommendations

Rank	Item	Source
Option	Shoulderpads of the Stranger Shoulderpads of the Stranger	Drop: Hydross the Unstable (Serpentshrine Cavern)

Best in Slot Back Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 4
Shadowmoon Destroyer's Drape is individually the best in slot cloak for Phase 4. However, gear sets using this cloak will eventually exceed the hit cap, so Thalassian Wildercloak is the optimal choice in a fully optimized gear set.

Best in Slot Chest Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 4
On its own, Nether Shadow Tunic is substantially better for Feral DPS than all other chest pieces in Phase 4. However, since the 4-piece Thunderheart Harness set bonus is so incredibly strong, Thunderheart Chestguard is the superior choice once you have acquired the remaining three Tier 6 pieces for activating the bonus. Prior to that point, it is optimal to keep wearing your Tier 4 chest instead in order to maintain the 2-piece Malorne Harness set bonus, which continues to scale well throughout Phase 4.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS Individually	Nether Shadow Tunic Nether Shadow Tunic	Drop: Supremus (Black Temple)

Option (with 4pT5)	Nordrassil Chestplate Nordrassil Chestplate	Drop: Kael'thas Sunstrider (Tempest Keep: The Eye)

If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Chest Armor Recommendations

Rank	Item	Source

Best in Slot Leg Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 4
As you may have guessed, Thunderheart Leggings are the highest DPS leg piece for cats in Phase 4, and are also best in slot for bear tanking. These legs should be your highest priority Tier 6 token to target first, since you will be able to immediately utilize them both in Cat and Bear Form. In contrast, the Tier 6 gloves are not utilized for cat DPS until you can activate the 2-piece Thunderheart Harness set bonus, and the Tier 6 shoulders + chest are not equipped until you obtain all four pieces, as it is not worth breaking the 2-piece Malorne Harness set bonus until that point.

However, these boots use up too much "hit budget" to fit into optimized gear sets, so Shadowmaster's Boots from Phase 3 remain the optimal choice in both 9% Hit and 6% Hit setups.

Best in Slot Neck Jewelry for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 4
When activated, Braided Eternium Chain is the highest DPS neckpiece for cats in Phase 4. Weapon damage is a particularly strong DPS stat for Feral Druids due to their fast 1.0 second attack speed. Moreover, once you account for the 3-4 additional party members who will also benefit from the bonus crit rating, the total raid DPS value of this neckpiece is miles above any alternatives.

In a competitive raid environment, two players in each physical DPS group should use this necklace to maintain full uptime on the crit buff, and Feral DPS Druids are a great candidate for this role in the Feral/Hunter/Warrior/Shaman group due to their exceptional weapon damage scaling. Unfortunately, the limited number of charges means that a new necklace will need to be crafted every few raids, making it function more like a consumable than a gear piece.

When Braided Eternium Chain is on cooldown, the best alternative necklace depends on your remaining itemization and on the raid encounter: Choker of Endless Nightmares provides the highest personal DPS, but uses up a lot of "hit budget" and is therefore difficult to fit into an optimized gear set. If you are already hit capped, then Choker of Serrated Blades is BiS when facing bosses with 6200 base Armor (such as all of the bosses in Hyjal Summit), whereas Telonicus's Pendant of Mayhem is the optimal choice when facing bosses with 7700 base Armor (which encompasses all of the Black Temple fights except for Teron Gorefiend, Mother Shahraz, and The Illidari Council).
Rank	Item	Source
BiS Individually	Choker of Endless Nightmares Choker of Endless Nightmares	Drop: Supremus (Black Temple)
BiS in 6% Hit Set	Telonicus's Pendant of Mayhem Telonicus's Pendant of Mayhem	Drop: Kael'thas Sunstrider (Tempest Keep: The Eye)
BiS in 6% Hit Set	Choker of Serrated Blades Choker of Serrated Blades	Drop: Trash Mobs (Black Temple / Hyjal Summit)
BiS for Raid DPS	Braided Eternium Chain Braided Eternium Chain	Profession: Jewelcrafting - can be purchased on the Auction House

Stormrage Signet Ring is, individually, the best available ring for Feral DPS in Phase 4, but it unfortunately uses up tons of hit budget and is difficult to fit into a fully optimized gear set. As a result, when raiding with a Balance Druid providing Improved Faerie Fire, a more practical ring pairing for Ferals in Phase 4 is Signet of Primal Wrath and Band of the Eternal Champion.

Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 4
The Zul'Aman raid brings with it a new BiS trinket option, Berserker's Call, which is a direct upgrade to Bloodlust Brooch / Empty Mug of Direbrew, and a sidegrade to Mark of the Champion (with the added advantage of working against all enemy types and benefiting from faster kill times).

The absolute best trinket pairing for cats in Phase 4 will always involve two of the following three trinkets: Tsunami Talisman, Madness of the Betrayer, and Berserker's Call. The choice of exactly which two to use depends on several factors, including fight length, boss armor, available hit budget in your gear set, and helm choice (Wolfshead vs. alternatives). However, all combinations of these three trinkets sim within +/- 5 DPS of one another, so don't stress out about equipping the "perfect" trinket pairing as long as you have any two of these.
Rank	Item	Source

Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 4
The only competitive weapons for Feral Druids throughout the entirety of TBC are two-handed staves and maces, so one-handed options need not be considered at all.

The Season 3 Arena weapon, Vengeful Gladiator's Staff, remains best in slot for individual DPS value in Phase 4, provided you have sufficient hit budget available in your gear set to efficiently utilize the hit Rating on it. However, the Zul'Aman raid introduces a strong alternative weapon option without any hit Rating on it - Staff of Primal Fury. Similar to trinket selection, the optimal weapon choice between these two options depends on whether or not your raid uses the Improved Faerie Fire debuff, whether or not it is your turn to equip Braided Eternium Chain Braided Eternium Chain, the other sources of hit Rating in your gear set, as well as boss armor.

As mentioned in the trinket section above, if you are not experienced with simming your own gear sets, then don't stress out about whether to swap from Vengeful Gladiator's Staff to Staff of Primal Fury in Phase 4; the DPS difference between the two is negligible, provided that you itemize the rest of your set around the presence or absence of hit rating in the weapon slot.

Note that if you have not ranked up competitively in Season 3 arenas and are still using the Season 2 weapon (Merciless Gladiator's Maul), then Staff of Primal Fury will be a substantial upgrade for you across all possible scenarios and should be permanently equipped when DPSing in Cat Form.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS Individually	Vengeful Gladiator's Staff Vengeful Gladiator's Staff	Vendor: Arena Points (Season 3)
BiS when Hit Capped	Staff of Primal Fury Staff of Primal Fury	Drop: Hex Lord Malacrass (Zul'Aman)
Option	Merciless Gladiator's Maul Merciless Gladiator's Maul	Vendor: Arena Points (Season 2)
Option	Pillar of Ferocity Pillar of Ferocity	Drop: Anetheron (Hyjal Summit)

If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:

Best in Slot Idols for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 4
For personal DPS, Everbloom Idol remains a clear winner in the Idol slot, and is a hefty upgrade over the pre-raid options that will last you through multiple content phases.

Phase 4 does introduce a new Idol option, Idol of Terror, but it is worse than Everbloom by around 12 DPS when wearing Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm. If you have dropped Wolfshead in favor of Cursed Vision of Sargeras Cursed Vision of Sargeras or Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm, then Idol of Terror is a lot more competitive, since the Primal Instinct buff will have much higher uptime when executing a Shred-Mangle rotation compared to a Shred-Shred rotation, but Everbloom Idol till comes out on top by about 3 DPS.

Note that when optimizing for your raid's overall performance rather than just your personal DPS parse, you should use Idol of the Raven Goddess instead of Everbloom whenever you are placed in a stacked physical DPS group that scales well with crit rating.
Rank	Item	Source

Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

Burning Crusade Classic Feral Druid DPS Best in Slot (BiS) Phase 5 Gear Guide
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/02/24
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Phase 5 Sunwell Plateau for Feral Druid DPS in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid DPS to acquire while they progress during the fifth and final phase of The Burning Crusade Classic, which contains gear sourced from Sunwell Plateau, Zul'Aman, Black Temple, and Mount Hyjal, plus other pieces from previous phases like Serpentshrine Cavern, Tempest Keep, heroic dungeons, arena PvP Gear, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.

Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid DPS in Phase 5
In this guide, recommendations in each slot are given in three different ways: the best piece individually in that slot, the optimal piece to use in a 9% hit raid set (hard hit cap), and the optimal piece to use in a 5-6% hit raid set (soft cap with Improved Faerie Fire and possibly Heroic Presence). These three categories are not always the same, as Phases 3 and 4 melee gear is generally overloaded with hit rating, so simply optimizing gear for individual DPS value leads to sets that far exceed the hit cap. For this reason, given two comparable pieces in a gear slot, the option with less hit on it is preferred as it frees up "hit budget" that you can use in other slots.

Feral Druids looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Gems & Enchants
Should Feral Druid DPS Choose Aldor or Scryer for BiS in TBC Classic Phase 5?
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryers faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For Feral DPS, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants: Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance and Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. Despite providing different distributions of attack Power versus critical strike rating, simulations show that both enchants provide identical DPS value to within the simulation resolution of +/- 1 DPS. So feel free to choose either faction based on personal preference, as the choice will not impact your DPS output whatsoever in the long run. Technically, these enchants are not even used in a true BiS gear set since Might of the Scourge Might of the Scourge is superior to both, but the vast majority of players will not have Sapphiron enchants saved up from Phase 6 of Classic.

In Phase 5, there is one new Feral DPS item with a meaningfully different behavior for the two factions: Shattered Sun Pendant of Might Shattered Sun Pendant of Might. The Aldor version of the proc, Light's Strength, provides 200 Attack Power for 10 seconds, which is substantially better than the Scryer version, Arcane Strike, which deals a static amount of spell damage that does not scale with your gear. Regardless of the proc, however, this neck is not competitive with the BiS options for cats in Phase 5, and is outclassed by superior raid drops like Choker of Endless Nightmares Choker of Endless Nightmares and Clutch of Demise Clutch of Demise. So feel free to choose either faction based on personal preference, as the choice will not impact your DPS performance whatsoever in the long run!

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
Wolfshead Helm remains a solid helm option for cat DPS in Phase 5, as the higher Shred cast frequency it enables scales with all of the other stat upgrades from Sunwell gear. However, Phase 5 marks the first time that conventional DPS helms will convincingly outperform Wolfshead across all raid environments and boss armor values.

Recall that the 2-piece bonus of Thunderheart Harness, which is retained in Phase 5 gear sets, opens up a new powershifting rotation in Cat Form that can work even without the extra 20 Energy per shift from Wolfshead. When wearing a conventional helm, simply replace the second Shred cast in a standard powershift cycle with a 35 Energy Mangle (Cat) cast instead, resulting in the same 4-second cycle time at the expense of reduced damage dealt:
Standard DPS Cycle with Wolfshead Helm	DPS Cycle without Wolfshead Helm using 2-piece Thunderheart Harness bonus
Time in seconds	Action/Event	Final Energy
0	Powershift	60
1	Energy Tick	80
1.5	GCD ends, cast Shred	38
2.5	GCD ends, wait	38
3	Energy Tick	58
3	Cast Shred	16
4	GCD ends, powershift	60
	
Time in seconds	Action/Event	Final Energy
0	Powershift	40
1	Energy Tick	60
1.5	GCD ends, cast Shred	18
2.5	GCD ends, wait	18
3	Energy Tick	38
3	Cast Mangle	3
4	GCD ends, powershift	40
Note that Mangle deals significantly less damage than Shred, which is why Wolfshead Helm was competitive with conventional DPS helms in Phases 3 and 4. In Phase 5, however, this damage difference is outweighed by the strong DPS stats and meta gem socket on conventional helms.

Assuming you do opt to forego Wolfshead, the optimal helms to use in Phase 5 are Duplicitous Guise and Thunderheart Cover in 9% and 6% hit sets, respectively. Depending on your other itemization, Cursed Vision of Sargeras can also be very competitive with the Tier 6 helm in 6% hit sets, provided you can efficiently utilize all of the hit rating on it.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS (9% Hit Set)	Duplicitous Guise Duplicitous Guise	Drop: Kil'jaeden (Sunwell Plateau)
BiS (6% Hit Set)	Thunderheart Cover Thunderheart Cover	Drop: Archimonde (Hyjal Summit)
Option	Cursed Vision of Sargeras Cursed Vision of Sargeras	Drop: Illidan Stormrage (Black Temple)
Option	Quad Deathblow X44 Goggles Quad Deathblow X44 Goggles	Profession: Engineering - BoP only

If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Head Armor Recommendations

Rank	Item	Source
Option	Brutal Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm Brutal Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm	Vendor: Arena Points (Season 4)
Option	Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm	Profession: Leatherworking - can be purchased on the Auction House

Best in Slot Shoulder Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
Demontooth Shoulderpads are individually the highest DPS shoulder piece for cats in Phase 5, and are also best in slot when tanking in Bear Form. However, if you are not using Thunderheart Cover Thunderheart Cover in the helm slot, then it is optimal to keep wearing your Tier 6 shoulders instead to maintain the 4-piece Thunderheart Harness set bonus, which continues to scale well throughout Phase 5.

Option	Shoulderpads of Vehemence Shoulderpads of Vehemence	Drop: Grand Warlock Alythess & Lady Sacrolash (Sunwell Plateau)

If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Shoulder Armor Recommendations

Rank	Item	Source

Best in Slot Back Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
Kil'jaeden drops the new best-in-slot cloak for Feral DPS in Phase 5, Cloak of Unforgivable Sin, which is a substantial upgrade from all alternative options but will be heavily contested by all physical DPS players.
Rank	Item	Source

Other Back Armor Recommendations

Rank	Item	Source
Option	Thalassian Wildercloak Thalassian Wildercloak	Drop: Kael'thas Sunstrider (Tempest Keep: The Eye)

Best in Slot Chest Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
Bladed Chaos Tunic is by far the highest DPS chest piece in Phase 5, with no competitive alternatives in the slot. Note that if you play an off-tank role in your raid team, you will likely be forced to choose between Bladed Chaos Tunic for DPS and Harness of Carnal Instinct for tanking, as the two items are sourced from the same raid drop.

One gearing strategy worth considering is using the Harness early on in the phase, when Ferals should tank almost every boss due to their much higher survivability relative to the other tank classes, and later trading it in for Tunic once the content is on farm and you can go back to DPSing most encounters.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS	Bladed Chaos Tunic Bladed Chaos Tunic	Vendor: Yrma
Drop: Harness of Carnal Instinct Harness of Carnal Instinct from M'uru (Sunwell Plateau) & Sunmote Sunmote
Option	Carapace of Sun and Shadow Carapace of Sun and Shadow	Profession: Leatherworking - BoP only
Option	Harness of Carnal Instinct Harness of Carnal Instinct	Drop: M'uru (Sunwell Plateau)
Option (with 4pT6)	Thunderheart Chestguard Thunderheart Chestguard	Drop: Illidan Stormrage (Black Temple)

If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:

Thunderheart Wristguards are individually BiS in the wrist slot, and are especially impactful because they allow you to equip Sunwell off-piece upgrades in other slots while maintaining the 4-piece Thunderheart Harness set bonus.

Best in Slot Hand Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
Gloves of Immortal Dusk are by far the best glove option for cat DPS in Phase 5, and should fortunately not be too difficult to obtain, as they can be crafted and are tradable.

Best in Slot Waist Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
Belt of One-Hundred Deaths is finally replaced in Phase 5 by Thunderheart Waistguard. On its own, the Tier 6 belt is only a minor upgrade from the Vashj belt, but it is still worth picking up as soon as you can, since it allows you to unlock much more impactful off-piece upgrades in other slots while maintaining the 4-piece Thunderheart Harness set bonus.

Best in Slot Leg Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
Of all the off-piece armor upgrades you can obtain in Sunwell Plateau, Leggings of the Immortal Night are the most impactful by a landslide, providing a whopping 110 DPS increase over the previously BiS Tier 6 legs.

Similar to the chest slot, Ferals playing an off-tank role in their raid team will be forced to pick between Leggings of the Immortal Night for cat DPS and Leggings of the Immortal Beast for bear tanking, with the bear version of the leggings performing around 66 DPS worse in Cat Form. Unlike the analogous chest pieces, however, you will not be able to convert the tank legs into DPS ones at a later date once the content is on farm, so make sure to plan out your choice in advance based on your raid's tanking assignments.
Rank	Item	Source

Best in Slot Feet Armor for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
The Phase 4 Badge of Justice boots, Nyn'jah's Tabi Boots, remain the highest DPS option in isolation for Feral Druids in Phase 5. However, the new Tier 6 boots, Thunderheart Treads, from Sunwell Plateau are less than 1 DPS worse, making them the obvious choice in a fully optimized gear set, as they allow you to access much more impactful upgrades in other slots while preserving the 4-piece Tier 6 set bonus. On top of this, the lack of hit rating on them frees up valuable hit budget that you can "spend" on other slots while staying below the relevant hit cap for your raid environment.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS (Individually)	Nyn'jah's Tabi Boots Nyn'jah's Tabi Boots	Vendor: G'eras (Badges of Justice)

Best in Slot Neck Jewelry for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
When activated, Braided Eternium Chain remains the highest value neckpiece for cats even in Phase 5 in terms of total raid DPS contribution. Weapon damage is a particularly strong DPS stat for Feral Druids due to their fast 1.0 second attack speed. Moreover, once you account for the 3-4 additional party members who will also benefit from the bonus crit rating, the total raid DPS value of this neckpiece is miles above any alternatives.

In a competitive raid environment, two players in each physical DPS group should use this necklace to maintain full uptime on the crit buff, and Feral DPS Druids are a great candidate for this role in the Feral/Hunter/Warrior/Shaman group due to their exceptional weapon damage scaling.

When Braided Eternium Chain is on cooldown, the best alternative necklace depends on your professions and on your remaining itemization.

Hard Khorium Choker provides the highest personal DPS, but it requires the Jewelcrafting profession to craft and equip. Note that if you are not already a Jewelcrafter, then it is not optimal to give up either Engineering or Enchanting for the sake of crafting a Khorium Choker, as the DPS increases provided by Sappers and ring enchants outweigh the DPS upgrade from the better neck.

For non-Jewelcrafters, the best options are Choker of Endless Nightmares and Clutch of Demise, which perform very similarly, provided that you itemize the rest of your gear set around the presence or absence of hit rating in the neck slot. Given their comparable DPS, the Supremus neck is the more practical choice between the two, as it will be far less contested and is a direct swap with the Eternium Chain (since it provides an identical amount of hit rating).
Rank	Item	Source
BiS (Individually)	Hard Khorium Choker Hard Khorium Choker	Profession: Jewelcrafting - BoP only
BiS (Raid DPS)	Braided Eternium Chain Braided Eternium Chain	Profession: Jewelcrafting - can be purchased on the Auction House
Option	Choker of Endless Nightmares Choker of Endless Nightmares	Drop: Supremus (Black Temple)
Option	Clutch of Demise Clutch of Demise	Drop: Brutallus (Sunwell Plateau)

If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:

Rank	Item	Source
Option	Choker of Serrated Blades Choker of Serrated Blades	Drop: Trash Mobs (Hyjal Summit)

Best in Slot Ring Jewelry for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
Stormrage Signet Ring is still individually the best available ring for Feral DPS in Phase 5, but it unfortunately uses up tons of hit budget and is difficult to fit into a fully optimized gear set. As a result, the optimal ring pairing for both 9% and 6% hit setups is Band of Ruinous Delight + Angelista's Revenge. It should be emphasized that Band of Ruinous Delight is a very low-priority pickup for Feral Druids relative to other classes, as it is only ~4 DPS better than Signet of Primal Wrath from Zul'Aman.
Rank	Item	Source

Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
Phase 5 brings with it two new BiS trinkets that both significantly outperform all alternatives from prior phases: Shard of Contempt from the Magisters' Terrace dungeon and Blackened Naaru Sliver from the Sunwell Plateau raid. If you are waiting for one or both of these trinkets to drop, then the next best option when decked out in Sunwell gear is Madness of the Betrayer, followed closely by Tsunami Talisman.

Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
The new Feral weapon from Sunwell Plateau, Stanchion of Primal Instinct, is by far the most impactful single upgrade that a Feral DPS Druid can obtain in Phase 5, providing over 200 additional DPS relative to the Phase 4 BiS weapons Vengeful Gladiator's Staff and Staff of Primal Fury. If you do not get lucky with an early Stanchion drop during your first couple of Sunwell Plateau lockouts, then it is worth picking up the new Badge of Justice reward Staff of the Forest Lord as an intermediate upgrade once the new Isle of Quel'Danas Badge vendor unlocks on your server.

Best in Slot Idols for Feral Druid DPS in TBC Classic Phase 5
For personal DPS, Everbloom Idol remains the clear winner in the Idol slot, but Idol of Terror is only about 2 DPS worse if you have dropped Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm and are using a Shred-Mangle rotation that allows for high uptime on the Primal Instinct buff.

Note that when optimizing for your raid's overall performance rather than just your personal DPS parse, you should use Idol of the Raven Goddess instead of Everbloom whenever you are placed in a stacked physical DPS group that scales well with crit rating.

Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.
