Feral Druid Tank Pre-Raid Best in Slot (BiS) - TBC Classic
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/01/29
In this Pre-Raid Best-in-Slot Gear List for Feral Druid Tank in The Burning Crusade Classic, we will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid Tank to aim for when gearing up to raids in The Burning Crusade Classic, including gear sourced from Normal and Heroic Dungeons, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.


Feral Druid Tank Best in Slot Hub
Pre-Raid Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 1
In this guide, the top recommended items in each slot will be pieces that can be either purchased with gold or acquired from questing, easily grinded reputation, or normal mode dungeons, even though there are usually better options from heroic dungeons or Badges of Justice. The reason for this is that Karazhan is known to be easier than pre-nerf heroic dungeons, and most players will run through Karazhan before farming heroics. Therefore, this guide aims to prepare players for their first foray into raiding using easily acquired gear pieces. For subsequent 5-man and PvP gear progression outside of raids, the full ranking of potential options is also provided below the recommended pre-raid piece.

Note on terminology: This guide will often classify gear options as the best "threat skewed," or the best "mit skewed" piece in a given slot. This does not necessarily mean that the piece has the highest possible TPS or max possible mitigation in that slot. Oftentimes, a gear piece with overall balanced threat and mitigation stats, but which leans one way or another, is the ideal tanking choice. For example, several of the optimal threat-skewed pieces will have slightly lower threat stats than pure DPS gear, but with a lot more Stamina.

The gear sets below represent example gear configurations for a fresh level 70 bear that are optimized to learn towards more mitigation or threat, respectively. It is important to emphasize that, unlike for DPS, the concept of "true BiS" does not apply to tank gearing, since different gear choices will be appropriate depending on the encounter, tanking role, and required threat ceiling for the top DPS in the group. As a result, you should not interpret the gear sets below as the only BiS configurations, but they serve as good templates for common scenarios you will encounter when first experiencing endgame TBC content.

Feral Druid Tanks looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Tank Gems & Enchants
Should Feral Druid Tank Choose Aldor or Scryer for BiS in TBC Classic Phase 1?
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For bear tanks, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants for threat and avoidance, respectively. The Aldor threat enchant is Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance, and the Scryer version is Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, both of which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. Despite providing different distributions of Attack Power versus Critical Strike Rating, calculations show that both enchants provide nearly identical TPS value within current modeling accuracy, so feel free to choose either faction based on personal preference.

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Tank Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Pre-Raid Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
Cobrascale Hood and Helm of the Claw are the best initial mit skewed and threat skewed helm options, respectively, for feral tanks before entering raids and heroics. When gearing on a budget, the latter is especially attractive as it is easily acquired from a single Steamvault run. There are some strong pieces from the Badge of Justice vendor; however, you should spend these on other items first, and Helm of the Claw is better.
Rank	Item	Source
Best - Mititgation	Cobrascale Hood Cobrascale Hood	Profession: Leatherworking
Best - Threat	Helm of the Claw Helm of the Claw	Quest:The Warlord's Hideout The Warlord's Hideout
Alternative	Mask of the Deceiver Mask of the Deceiver	Vendor: G'eras (50 Badges of Justice)
Alternative	Cowl of Beastly Rage Cowl of Beastly Rage	Vendor: G'eras (50 Badges of Justice)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:

Rank	Item	Source
Alternative	Wastewalker Helm Wastewalker Helm	Drop: Epoch Hunter (Heroic Old Hillsbrad Foothills)

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Shoulder Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
For threat-skewed sets, Mantle of Perenolde and Shoulderpads of Assassination remain great balanced options before raids and heroics. Since both these pieces are also pre-raid BiS for cat DPS, Agility gems are recommended over Stamina gems so that they can be used in both roles.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Mantle of Perenolde Mantle of Perenolde	Drop: Epoch Hunter (Old Hillsbrad Foothills)
Best	Shoulderpads of Assassination Shoulderpads of Assassination	Drop: Talon King Ikiss (Sethekk Halls)
Alternative	Mantle of Shadowy Embrace Mantle of Shadowy Embrace	Drop: Keli'dan the Breaker (Heroic The Blood Furnace)
Alternative	Wastewalker Shoulderpads Wastewalker Shoulderpads	Drop: Avatar of the Martyred (Heroic Auchenai Crupts)
Pre-Raid Best in Slot Back Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
Thoriumweave Cloak is a clear winner in the cloak slot for pre-raid gear progression. The Stamina and Armor levels on this cloak are so high that even a threat-skewed gear set would use it. For crit immune gear sets, Resolute Cape is a very comparable crafted option that also provides a ton of Resilience.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Thoriumweave Cloak Thoriumweave Cloak	Drop: Mechano-Lord Capacitus (The Mechanar)

Alternative	Auchenai Death Shroud Auchenai Death Shroud	Drop: Avatar of the Martyred (Heroic Auchenai Crypts)

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Chest Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
Heavy Clefthoof Vest is the recommended pre-raid chest piece to get prior to raids and heroics, and it should be readily available on the auction house for cheap due to the prevalence of leatherworkers in TBC.

When itemizing for higher threat, Tunic of Assassination is an easily accessible option that is also an excellent pre-raid DPS chest. The absolute best pre-raid threat piece outside of PvP is technically Primalstrike Vest, but it is strongly recommended not to invest your gold in crafting this. The mats for the Primalstrike set are extremely expensive, and they does not last long enough to justify the investment, being quickly replaced by raid drops.
Rank	Item	Source
Best - Mitigation	Heavy Clefthoof Vest Heavy Clefthoof Vest	Profession: Leatherworking
Best - Threat	Tunic of Assassination Tunic of Assassination	Drop: Pathaleon the Calculator (The Mechanar)
Alternative	Primalstrike Vest Primalstrike Vest	Profession: Elemental Leatherworking
Alternative	Wastewalker Tunic Wastewalker Tunic	Drop: Keli'dan the Breaker (Heroic The Blood Furnace)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Chest Armor Recommendations


Rank	Item	Source
Alternative	Ghoul Skin Tunic Ghoul Skin Tunic	Drop: Naxxramas Trash Mobs

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Wrist Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
While you are working on farming the required Marks and Honor for the PvP bracers (note that the Alliance version is linked here, but the Horde version has the exact same stats), the recommended early alternative is the quest reward Umberhowl's Collar.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Umberhowl's Collar Umberhowl's Collar	Quest: The Cipher of Damnation - The Third Fragment Recovered The Cipher of Damnation - The Third Fragment Recovered

Pre-Raid Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
The only competitive weapons for feral druids throughout the entirety of TBC are two-handed staves and maces, so one-handed options need not be considered at all. Bears should farm Earthwarden as soon as possible, ideally in parallel with the leveling process, as it is not only the strongest pre-raid tanking weapon, but can still be used even with raid gear as an efficient Defense swap for uncrittability.

For personal TPS, Idol of Brutality and Idol of the Wild can both be best-in-slot tanking Idols depending on the encounter. The former is preferred whenever the boss hits hard enough that you have enough available rage to sustain more than ~50% Maul uptime, with the latter being optimal otherwise.

Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/05/06 (Burning Crusade)
There is no requirement whatsoever to be hit capped. Hit is just a threat stat that is worth 2.7 AP in value, no more no less. The only difference between hit and other threat stats like AP or crit is that it provides 0 value above the cap. So you should never think of hit cap as a goal to hit, but rather as a limit that you should never exceed. As long as you are under the cap, just treat 1 Hit Rating exactly the same as 2.7 AP when evaluating gear. Most optimal tank sets in TBC will be well under the hit cap, and will put out more threat than sets that use suboptimal gear just to cap.

7

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/05/18 (Burning Crusade)
Let's suppose you cast 100 Mauls in a fight, each of which adds 10 Threat (numbers totally made up). So if you have a 0% chance to miss, you will generate 1000 total Threat. Now let's suppose that we make gear trades that drop Hit Rating in exchange for AP, so that we now have a 5% chance to miss but our Mauls now add 12 Threat from the increased damage done. Now our total Threat is 0.95 x 12 x 100 = 1140. So our TPS increased even though we miss more often. This is why hit always has an AP equivalency, you can always trade one for the other.

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/06/21 (Burning Crusade)
You never have to gem to match the socket color, matching the colors is only required to unlock the socket bonus on the gear piece. In the majority of cases, the socket bonus is not strong enough to compensate the loss of value incurred by using subpar gems just to match the socket color. For pre-raid gear, Stamina gems provide so much value that it is rarely worth it to deviate from them unless a piece has a really good bonus.

Feral Druid Tank Phase 1 Best in Slot (BiS) - TBC Classic
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/06/29
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Karazhan, Gruul's Lair, and Magtheridon's Lair for Feral Druid Tank in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid Tank to acquire while they progress on the first few raids in The Burning Crusade Classic, and contains gear sourced from Karazhan, Gruul's Lair, Magtheridon's Lair, heroic dungeons, early PvP grinding, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.

We have created many different best-in-slot lists, including a pre-raid list, containing gear you can obtain without doing any raid content.


Feral Druid Tank Best in Slot Guides
Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 1
Note on terminology: This guide will often classify gear options as the best "threat skewed," or the best "mit skewed" piece in a given slot. This does not necessarily mean that the piece has the highest possible TPS or max possible mitigation in that slot. Oftentimes, a gear piece with overall balanced threat and mitigation stats, but which leans one way or another, is the ideal tanking choice. For example, several of the optimal threat-skewed pieces will have slightly lower threat stats than pure DPS gear, but with a lot more Stamina.

Note on gearing assumptions: The recommendations in this guide are conservative choices, with survivability generally prioritized over threat. This is based on the assumption that raid bosses in TBC hit hard enough that heavy mitigation gear is required to reliably survive these encounters. If learnings from the TBC beta indicate that boss damage is lower than expected, or that threat demands are more stringent than expected, these lists will be updated to skew more heavily towards threat stats (similar to vanilla gearing).

Note on gemming: Based on the above assumptions, Stamina gems are generally recommended in every slot as they provide the most efficient EHP increase in a single slot. However, certain gear pieces will be listed with gems of other colors so that the overall gear set will meet the color requirements of the Relentless Earthstorm Diamond Relentless Earthstorm Diamond Meta gem.

Feral Druid Tanks looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Tank Gems & Enchants
Should Feral Druid Tank Choose Aldor or Scryer for BiS in TBC Classic Phase 1?
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For bear tanks, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants for threat and avoidance, respectively. The Aldor threat enchant is Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance, and the Scryer version is Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, both of which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. Despite providing different distributions of Attack Power versus Critical Strike Rating, calculations show that both enchants provide nearly identical TPS value within current modeling accuracy.

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Tank Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic

Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
Stag-Helm of Malorne is the highest overall value tanking helm for bears in Phase 1, and is massively better than alternatives. The upgrade from pre-raid gear to the Tier 4 helm is so large that competitive raiding guilds should prioritize the helm token for feral tanks. Note that while green gems are generally very inefficient for bears, it is recommended in this slot to meet the Meta gem requirements for Relentless Earthstorm Diamond Relentless Earthstorm Diamond.
Rank	Item	Source
Best Overall	Stag-Helm of Malorne Stag-Helm of Malorne	Drop: Tier Token from Prince Malchezaar (Karazhan)
Best Threat	Malefic Mask of the Shadows Malefic Mask of the Shadows	Drop: High King Maulgar (Gruul's Lair)
Mit skewed PvP alternative	Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm	1875 Arena Points Arena Points - Arena Vendor
Threat skewed alternative	Mask of the Deceiver Mask of the Deceiver	Vendor: G'eras (50 Badges of Justice)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Head Armor Recommendations


Rank	Item	Source
Threat skewed alternative	Cowl of Defiance Cowl of Defiance	Drop: Netherspite (Karazhan)

Best in Slot Shoulder Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
Mantle of Malorne is the best shoulder piece for both cat DPS and bear tanking in Phase 1, and should be relatively easy to acquire as a tier piece.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Mantle of Malorne Mantle of Malorne	Drop: Tier Token from High King Maulgar (Gruul's Lair)
Mit skewed alternative	Gladiator's Dragonhide Spaulders Gladiator's Dragonhide Spaulders	1500 Arena Points Arena Points - Arena Vendor
Threat skewed alternative	Bladed Shoulderpads of the Merciless Bladed Shoulderpads of the Merciless	Drop: Echo of Medivh (Karazhan Chess Event)
Alternative	Mantle of Shadowy Embrace Mantle of Shadowy Embrace	Drop: Keli'dan the Breaker (Heroic The Blood Furnace)

Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
Early TBC brings in a diverse array of potential trinket options, with many efficient choices depending on your professions, threat versus survivability needs, and access to vanilla loot. Just as in vanilla, Mark of the Champion is the best in slot threat trinket by a large margin for all TBC undead/demon encounters in Phase 1, which encompasses about half of the bosses in Karazhan as well as Magtheridon. For bosses that are neither undead nor demon, Dragonspine Trophy is a clear winner and can last for many phases, but is highly contested by all physical DPS classes. The next best threat trinket option is Bloodlust Brooch, which is the second most important Badge of Justice Badge of Justice reward for feral druids to prioritize after Everbloom Idol Everbloom Idol.

Update: Several readers have inquired about the absence of Moroes' Lucky Pocket Watch as one of the BiS options mentioned above. Contrary to popular belief, this trinket is actually fairly mediocre at Tier 4 gear levels. On-use Dodge trinkets provide the most value when the passive avoidance of the tank is already very high, due to the increasing returns of avoidance towards survivability.
Rank	Item	Source
Best Threat Option	Mark of the Champion Mark of the Champion	Quest: The Fall of Kel'Thuzad The Fall of Kel'Thuzad - The Phylactery of Kel'Thuzad The Phylactery of Kel'Thuzad from Kel'Thuzad (Naxramas)
Best Threat Option	Dragonspine Trophy Dragonspine Trophy	Drop: Gruul the Dragonkiller (Gruul's Lair)
Best Threat Option	Bloodlust Brooch Bloodlust Brooch	Vendor: G'eras (41 Badges of Justice)
Best Survivability Option	Goblin Rocket Launcher Goblin Rocket Launcher / Gnomish Poultryizer Gnomish Poultryizer	Profession: Engineering - (BoE, Requires 350 Engineering skill)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:

Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 1
Terestian's Stranglestaff is the best overall tanking weapon in Phase 1, and is also best in slot for cat DPS. However, Gladiator's Maul is close enough to Stranglestaff in overall value that it ends up being the optimal choice in defensively oriented gear sets that aim for uncrittability, as it is one of the most efficient available Defense swaps in the tier. For pure mitigation, Earthwarden reigns supreme, and can be swapped in mid-fight as a proxy for a defensive cooldown, given its extremely high armor value.
Rank	Item	Source
Best Overall	Terestian's Stranglestaff Terestian's Stranglestaff	Drop: Terestian Illhoof (Karazhan)
Best Defense Swap	Gladiator's Maul Gladiator's Maul	1875 Arena Points Arena Points - Arena Vendor
Best Mitigation	Earthwarden Earthwarden	Vendor: Fedryen Swiftspear Requires Exalted with Cenarion Expedition
Alternative	Feral Staff of Lashing Feral Staff of Lashing	Drop: Warp Splinter - (Heroic The Botanica)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Weapon Recommendations

Rank	Item	Source
Alternative	Staff of Natural Fury Staff of Natural Fury	World Drop - Outland

Idols for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 1
Idol of Brutality and Idol of the Wild can both be best-in-slot tanking Idols depending on the encounter. Idol of Brutality is preferred whenever the boss hits hard enough that you have enough available rage to sustain more than ~50% Maul uptime, with Idol of the Wild being optimal otherwise.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Idol of Brutality Idol of Brutality	Drop: Magistrate Barthilas (Stratholme)
Best	Idol of the Wild Idol of the Wild	Quest: Colossal Menace Colossal Menace
Situational	Gladiator's Idol of Resolve Gladiator's Idol of Resolve	500 Arena Points Arena Points - Arena Vendor
Distant Second	Idol of Ursoc Idol of Ursoc	Drop: Hungarfen (Heroic The Underbog)
NerdEgghead
Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

By frosty11161 (6,717 – 2·9·12) on 2021/06/25 (Burning Crusade)

2

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/06/30 (Burning Crusade)
There is no real point doing this, because feral gearing is absolutely atrocious without at least 1 and preferably 2-3 PvP offset pieces each phase. You have to use very inefficient swaps like the Defense shoulder enchant, Resilience enchants and gems, and would likely be forced to always equip Earthwarden rather than the two superior balanced choices. Feral tanks should view at least the PvP bracers each phase as a mandatory requirement to tank effectively. The good news is that the honor and marks can be pre-farmed in the previous phase, so you have plenty of time to prepare yourself for P2.

By Hadrial (5,240 – 2·21·29) on 2021/07/06 (Burning Crusade)	
	
Could you provide a conversion table of resilience to defense, so we can calculate our mixed gear easily? Rather than finding out the hard way. The Gladiator 2h will be very difficult to obtain in phase 1, as well as much of the pvp gear if you face horde bg wait times.

Thanks.

1

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/07/07 (Burning Crusade)
Each Resilience Rating reduces your chance to be critically hit by 0.0254%, and each Defense Rating reduces your chance to be critically hit by 0.0169%. So for crit immunity, 1 Resilience Rating is worth the same as 1.5 Defense Rating. If you visit the #feral-tank channel of the Druid Class Discord, we have a full table pinned of the possible Defense + Resilience combinations that can get you crit immunity.

1

The stat upgrade from T4 to T5 pieces more than outweighs the benefit from the 2-piece and 4-piece T4 bonuses, so you will want to wear full T5 gear in Phase 2.

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/08/02 (Burning Crusade)
As a reference point, you would need around 65% raid buffed avoidance for the pocket watch to be competitive with the Engineering Stamina trinkets for survivability. By Galgrom (241 – 1·5) on 2021/11/26 (Burning Crusade)	
	
You mention Romulo's Poison Vial Romulo's Poison Vial in the P2 guide as BiS, but it is not mentioned in the P1 guide. Is there any reason for that? Does the trinket become significantly better in P2, due to some other gear availability?
Last edited by Galgrom on 2021/11/26

0

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/12/31 (Burning Crusade)
When the P1 guide was written, our calculations at the time suggested that Romulo's Poison Vial was not competitive with alternative options for TPS. In P2, we identified errors in the way that Rage returns from misses were being modeled, as well as in the calculation of the proc DPS. Once we corrected these errors, RPV sims at comparable TPS to DST, provided you can make use of all of the Hit Rating on it. This conclusion holds in Phase 1 as well, we just didn't know it at the time 🙂

Feral Druid Tank Best in Slot (BiS) Phase 2 Gear Guide - TBC Classic
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/05/12
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Tempest Keep and Serpentshrine Cavern for Feral Druid Tank in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid Tank to acquire while they progress during the second phase of The Burning Crusade Classic, and contains gear sourced from Tempest Keep and Serpentshrine Cavern, plus other pieces from previous phases like Karazhan, Gruul's Lair, Magtheridon's Lair, heroic dungeons, early PvP grinding, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.

We have created many different best in slot lists, including a TBCC pre-patch list and a pre-raid list, containing gear you can obtain without doing any raid content.


Feral Druid Tank Best in Slot HubFeral Druid Tank Pre-Raid Best in Slot
Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 2
Note on terminology: This guide will often classify gear options as the best "threat skewed," or the best "mit skewed" piece in a given slot. This does not necessarily mean that the piece has the highest possible TPS or max possible mitigation in that slot. Oftentimes, a gear piece with overall balanced threat and mitigation stats, but which leans one way or another, is the ideal tanking choice. For example, several of the optimal threat-skewed pieces will have slightly lower threat stats than pure DPS gear, but with a lot more Stamina. It is important to emphasize that, unlike for DPS, the concept of "true BiS" does not apply for tank gearing, since different gear choices will be appropriate depending on the raid encounter, tanking role, and required threat ceiling for the top DPS in the group.

Note on gearing assumptions: The recommendations in this guide are conservative choices, with survivability generally prioritized over threat. This is based on the assumption that raid bosses in TBC hit hard enough that heavy mitigation gear is required to reliably survive these encounters. If learnings from the TBC beta indicate that boss damage is lower than expected, or that threat demands are more stringent than expected, these lists will be updated to skew more heavily towards threat stats (similar to vanilla gearing).

Feral Druid Tanks looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Tank Gems & Enchants

Should Feral Druid Tank Choose Aldor or Scryer for BiS in TBC Classic Phase 2?
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For bear tanks, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants for threat and avoidance, respectively. The Aldor threat enchant is Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance, and the Scryer version is Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, both of which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. Despite providing different distributions of Attack Power versus Critical Strike Rating, calculations show that both enchants provide nearly identical TPS value within current modeling accuracy.

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Tank Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 2
Nordrassil Headdress is the highest overall value tanking helm for bears, and is a sizable upgrade from the Tier 4 helm. However, on any fight where you are not tanking 100% of the time, then wearing Wolfshead Helm is mandatory so that you can powershift effectively in Cat Form when not actively tanking.

Best in Slot Waist Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 2
Belt of One-Hundred Deaths is such a massive threat upgrade over all alternative Phase 2 belts that it is optimal even in heavily mit skewed sets. Generally speaking, tanks in TBC should shy away from contesting DPS gear since there is plenty of well-itemized uncontested tank gear available. However, this belt in particular is a special case where priority to bear tanks can be justified, since tanks get twice the value from Expertise as DPS when attacking from the front.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Belt of One-Hundred Deaths Belt of One-Hundred Deaths	Drop: Lady Vashj (Serpentshrine Cavern)
Alternative	Belt of Natural Power Belt of Natural Power	Profession: Leatherworking
Alternative	Belt of Deep Shadow Belt of Deep Shadow	Profession: Leatherworking
Alternative	Tree-Mender's Belt Tree-Mender's Belt	Drop: Reinforced Fel Iron Chest (Heroic Hellfire Ramparts)]

Best in Slot Feet Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 2
Boots of Natural Grace are individually the highest value boots for feral tanks, providing an efficient balance of threat and mitigation stats, but are BoP and require the Leatherworking profession to craft. However, Boots of Natural Grace are the optimal choice in most gear sets, since they are a very efficient crit immunity swap.

Best in Slot Ring Jewelry for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 2
Just as in Phase 1, Violet Signet of the Great Protector and A'dal's Signet of Defense are the best ring pairing to use for mitigation sets.

When itemizing for higher threat, Shapeshifter's Signet, Band of the Ranger-General, and Ring of Lethality are all equivalently good options, and you can swap in either one or two of these based on your needs.

Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 2
Early TBC brings in a diverse array of potential trinket options, with many efficient choices depending on your professions, threat versus survivability needs, and access to vanilla loot. The absolute best trinket pairing for the majority of encounters is Badge of Tenacity combined with either Romulo's Poison Vial or Dragonspine Trophy, both providing near identical TPS as long as all of the Hit Rating on Romulo's Poison Vial is utilized. The first trinket mentioned is well worth the gold or farming time required to obtain it, as it provides twice as much value as any other trinket option. The passive mitigation from the static armor on this trinket is enormous, and the on-use doubles as both a significant threat and avoidance cooldown.

Rank	Item	Source
Best	Badge of Tenacity Badge of Tenacity	Depleted Badge Depleted Badge, 50 Apexis Shard Apexis Shard (Blades Edge Mountains)
Best	Romulo's Poison Vial Romulo's Poison Vial	Drop: Julianne (Karazhan)
Best	Dragonspine Trophy Dragonspine Trophy	Drop: Gruul the Dragonkiller (Gruul's Lair)
Threat Alternative	Tsunami Talisman Tsunami Talisman	Drop: Leotheras the Blind (Serpentshrine Cavern)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:

Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 2
Wildfury Greatstaff is the best PvE feral weapon introduced, but is unfortunately itemized in an unbalanced way to skew primarily towards mitigation stats. As a result, feral tanks interested in a more threat-oriented weapon will be forced to compete in Season 2 Arenas to obtain Merciless Gladiator's Maul.
Rank	Item	Source
Best mit skewed	Wildfury Greatstaff Wildfury Greatstaff	Drop: Serpentshrine Cavern Trash Mobs
Best threat skewed	Merciless Gladiator's Maul Merciless Gladiator's Maul	Arena Points Arena Points - Arena Vendor
Alternative	Terestian's Stranglestaff Terestian's Stranglestaff	Drop: Terestian Illhoof (Karazhan)
Alternative	Gladiator's Maul Gladiator's Maul	Honor Points Honor Points - Arena Vendor

Idols for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 2
For personal TPS, Idol of Brutality and Idol of the Wild can both be best-in-slot tanking Idols depending on the encounter. Idol of Brutality is preferred whenever the boss hits hard enough that you have enough available rage to sustain more than ~50% Maul uptime, with Idol of the Wild being optimal otherwise. However, since the Swift Flight Form quest chain appears to be arriving earlier than expected, Idol of the Raven Goddess will also be available as a quest reward from the chain. This Idol is a significant personal TPS loss compared to the other two, but provides a lot of raid DPS by buffing your entire party. In a competitive raid environment, you should pretty much always use Raven Goddess over the aforementioned when placed in a physical group.
Rank	Item	Source
Best	Idol of the Raven Goddess Idol of the Raven Goddess	Quest:Vanquish the Raven God Vanquish the Raven God
Personal TPS Alternative	Idol of Brutality Idol of Brutality	Drop: Magistrate Barthilas (Stratholme)
Alternative	Idol of the Wild Idol of the Wild	Quest: Colossal Menace Colossal Menace
Alternative	Idol of Ursoc Idol of Ursoc	Drop: Hungarfen (Heroic The Underbog)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Idol Recommendations

Rank	Item	Source

NerdEgghead
Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

By Lotimar (17,501 – 2·10·16) on 2021/09/16 (Burning Crusade)	
	
What about Romulo's Poison Vial Romulo's Poison Vial. It is really good for threat. I don't even see it considered.

3

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/09/23 (Burning Crusade)
Romulo's Poison Vial was previously not included in the BiS lists because sims predicted that its TPS contribution was quite mediocre. However, quite recently, errors were identified in how Rage returns were being modeled, resulting in a higher value for Hit Rating than previously estimated. Furthermore, the TPS value of the proc was also determined to be higher than previously estimated. Because of this, Romulo is actually best in slot for threat, providing pretty much identical TPS as DST! I've updated the guide to reflect these new sim conclusions.

0

	

Geldan on 2021/09/23 (Burning Crusade)
How significant is the threat from the proc? Wouldn't the value for rage return be significantly diminished if you are in a tanking situation where you already have enough rage to hit maul on every auto even when you miss a couple times in a row?

0

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/09/23 (Burning Crusade)
The proc provides around 13 TPS on average, which is equivalent to about 58 AP in value. And yes you're absolutely right, Rage return from misses stops having any influence on TPS when you're fully Rage capped with 100% Maul uptime. So in fully Rage capped situations, the value of Hit Rating is even higher than it is when partially Rage starved, since there's no longer an "upside" from missing a Mangle or Lacerate.

By antariaterrara (288 – 7·1) on 2021/08/20 (Burning Crusade)	
	
How critical is the upgrade to T5 from T4 / How worth is the Lacerate TPS in T5 compared to defensiveness of the T4?

got 4 piece already, wondering how much should I be fighting the rest of the healers for these tokens :D

1

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/08/24 (Burning Crusade)
Tier 5 is an absolutely enormous upgrade over Tier 4 on all axes: threat, EHP, and avoidance. But the reason isn't the set bonus - the Lacerate buff adds a very insignificant amount of TPS. The individual pieces are just much too good to pass up. If you have 4-piece T4, then you should break the set if you acquire either the Tier 5 legs, or any combination of two T5 pieces. At those breakpoints, the raw stat upgrades from Tier 5 will outweigh the Tier 4 bonuses. My suggestion is for tanks to request early priority for Tier 5, but then pass most off-piece upgrades to DPS.

0

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/09/23 (Burning Crusade)
Yes you would use the Brewfest Stamina trinkets as a direct swap-in for the Engineering trinkets. The on-use effects of the Engineering trinkets aren't helpful for Feral tanks because they have a long cast time and require being out of form. So the Brewfest versions are just strictly better because they provide the same Stamina but with some added DPS from the summoned guardian.

1

	
By Shadowbands (3,502 – 7·14) on 2021/10/31 (Burning Crusade)	
	
Any thoughts on The 2 Ring? I just got one and unsure whether to AH or not

1

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/11/17 (Burning Crusade)
The 2 Ring is a strong entry level PvE ring for Ferals, and is listed as the top option in the updated pre-raid BiS guide for Phase 2. However, it is significantly outclassed by raid drops, which is why it does not show up in the overall Phase 2 BiS guide. Given its sky high AH value, I would recommend keeping it only if you intend to PvP somewhat seriously as Feral.

0

	
By frosty11161 (6,717 – 2·9·12) on 2021/09/15 (Burning Crusade)	
	
How big of a difference is the Wildfury Greatstaff vs Earthwarden?

I'm thinking with combination of the Belt of One-Hundred Deaths, that's 3% worth of expertise, which coupled with some basic hit is going to be HUGE for TPS.

1

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2021/09/15 (Burning Crusade)
Wildfury is a huge upgrade over Earthwarden, providing around 250 AP equivalent in value when you consider both the threat and mitigation increase. So there is no circumstance in which you should ever consider Earthwarden over Wildfury for tanking, you can safely delete it once you get the SSC staff. Even for just pure TPS alone, Wildfury provides around 35 more TPS than Earthwarden - the AP upgrade is worth far more than the loss of Expertise. Remember that while Expertise is a great threat stat, it has a finite value that you need to compare to the value of other threat stats like Attack Power. In this case, the AP upgrade wins by a landslide.

-3

Burning Crusade Classic Feral Druid Tank Best in Slot (BiS) Phase 3 Gear Guide
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/07/31
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Phase 3 Black Temple and Mount Hyjal for Feral Druid Tank in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid Tank to acquire while they progress during the third phase of The Burning Crusade Classic, and contains gear sourced from Black Temple and Mount Hyjal, plus other pieces from previous phases like Serpentshrine Cavern, Tempest Keep, heroic dungeons, arena PvP Gear, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.
Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 3
Note on terminology: This guide will often classify gear options as the best "Threat skewed" or the best "mit skewed" piece in a given slot. This does not necessarily mean that the piece has the highest possible TPS or max possible mitigation in that slot. Oftentimes, a gear piece with overall balanced Threat and mitigation stats, but which leans one way or another, is the ideal tanking choice. For example, several of the optimal Threat-skewed pieces will have slightly lower Threat stats than pure DPS gear, but with a lot more Stamina.

Note on gemming: In our Phase 1 and Phase 2 BiS guides, Stamina gems have generally been recommended as the default option in all slots except when gearing for beneficial socket bonuses or to meet the color requirements of the Relentless Earthstorm Diamond Relentless Earthstorm Diamond meta gem. In Phase 3, however, the overall avoidance levels on Feral gear get high enough that Agility gems come out on top as the most efficient default choice due to the increasing returns of avoidance towards survivability. Nevertheless, all of the available Epic quality tanking gems in Phase 3 are close enough in value that it is almost always worth gemming for socket bonuses using a mixture of Delicate Crimson Spinel Delicate Crimson Spinel, Shifting Shadowsong Amethyst Shifting Shadowsong Amethyst, and Glinting Pyrestone Glinting Pyrestone gems.

Note that, unlike all prior phases of Classic WoW (both vanilla and TBC), Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm is no longer required for hybrid off-tank gear sets starting in Phase 3! This is because the 2-piece bonus of Thunderheart Harness opens up a new powershifting rotation in Cat Form that can work even without the extra 20 Energy per shift from Wolfshead.

Feral Druid Tanks looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Tank Gems & Enchants

Aldor vs. Scryer for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 3
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For bear tanks, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants for threat and avoidance, respectively. The Aldor threat enchant is Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance, and the Scryer version is Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, both of which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. Despite providing different distributions of Attack Power versus Critical Strike Rating, calculations show that both enchants provide nearly identical TPS value within current modeling accuracy.

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Tank Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 3
Thunderheart Cover and Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm are the two highest overall value tanking helms for bears in Phase 3, and both are sizable upgrades from the Tier 5 helm. The Tier 6 helm is generally the better choice when main tanking due to its superior mitigation stats, while the Arena helm is preferable when off-tanking due to its impressive DPS stats for when you are in Cat Form, or when prioritizing your Threat output against weakly hitting mobs.
Rank	Item	Source
Best Mit Skewed	Thunderheart Cover Thunderheart Cover	Drop: Helm of the Forgotten Vanquisher - Archimonde (Hyjal Summit)
Best Threat Skewed	Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm	1875 Arena Points Arena Points
Alternative	Cursed Vision of Sargeras Cursed Vision of Sargeras	Drop: Illidan Stormrage (Black Temple)
Alternative	Nordrassil Headdress Nordrassil Headdress	Drop: Helm of the Vanquished Defender - Lady Vashj(Serpentshrine Cavern)

Best in Slot Chest Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 3
Similar to the other Tier pieces, Thunderheart Chestguard is a direct stat upgrade from Nordrassil Chestplate. Note that the chest slot is a great place to fill in the missing color requirements for the Relentless Earthstorm Diamond Relentless Earthstorm Diamond meta gem due to the strong socket bonus, and a variety of gem choices can be appropriate depending on how often you tank vs. DPS in raids.

Best in Slot Wrist Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 3
The epic PvP off-set bracers from Season 3 are the best tanking bracers for the entirety of Phase 3, just as the Season 1-2 bracers were for Phase 1-2. In Season 3, the "Dragonhide" version of the PvP off-set is given a complete stat overhaul, removing the +Healing stats on it in exchange for valuable Critical Strike Rating. As a result, Vindicator's Dragonhide Bracers also become the best in slot cat DPS bracers for Phase 3, making them especially important to acquire for Feral off-tanks. Note that Insidious Bands are better bear tanking bracers individually, but the difference is small enough that the PvP bracers are the very first piece to swap in when building a crit immune gear set.
Rank	Item	Source

Best in Slot Neck Jewelry for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 3
On its own, Telonicus's Pendant of Mayhem is a substantially better neckpiece than all available alternatives. However, similar to the bracer slot, the Season 3 PvP off-set necklace is a superior tanking option in any fully optimized gear set, since it is an incredibly efficient source of crit immunity. If not all of the 18 Resilience on the PvP neck will be efficiently utilized, which is often the case in defensively leaning gear sets, then Pendant of Titans is an alternative swap that can be made instead.

Best in Slot Ring Jewelry for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 3
Phase 3 marks the first time that the double Armor ring pairing of Violet Signet of the Great Protector and A'dal's Signet of Defense is no longer optimal, even in heavy mitigation sets. This is because the Feral Tier 6 set packs so much bonus Armor that wearing Phase 3 gear will be nearly armor-capped with only a single defensive ring equipped. As a result, it is recommended to use Ring of Deceitful Intent instead in your second ring slot, as it is the best threat-skewed ring option available in Phase 3, packing a ton of Stamina on top of its impressive DPS stats.
Rank	Item	Source
Best Overall	Violet Signet of the Great Protector Violet Signet of the Great Protector	Quest:Path of the Violet Protector Path of the Violet Protector - Requires Exalted with The Violet Eye
Best Overall	Ring of Deceitful Intent Ring of Deceitful Intent	Drop: Shade of Akama (Black Temple)
Balanced Alternative	Band of the Eternal Champion Band of the Eternal Champion	Quest: Champion's Covenant Champion's Covenant - Requires Exalted with Scale of the Sands
Threat Alternative	Stormrage Signet Ring Stormrage Signet Ring	Drop: Illidan Stormrage (Black Temple)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Ring Jewelry Recommendations

Rank	Item	Source

Mitigation Alternative	A'dal's Signet of Defense A'dal's Signet of Defense	Quest: The Fall of Magtheridon The Fall of Magtheridon/The Fall of Magtheridon The Fall of Magtheridon from Magtheridon's Head Magtheridon's Head (Magtheridon's Lair)

Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 3
Phase 3 of TBC introduces a new class of on-use health trinkets, which we will refer to as "Last Stand trinkets" due to their similarity to the Warrior Warrior  Last Stand ability. These trinkets serve as powerful defensive cooldowns that can easily save your life when used immediately after a burst damage spike from a raid boss or trash mob. If you opt not to use a double Last Stand trinket pairing, then the best alternative options are the same as in Phase 2: Badge of Tenacity for mitigation, Bloodlust Brooch for burst Threat, and any of our previous recommended BiS threat trinkets for overall threat.
Rank	Item	Source
Best Defensive Cooldown	Shadowmoon Insignia Shadowmoon Insignia	Drop: Gurtogg Bloodboil (Black Temple)
Best Defensive Cooldown	Battlemaster's Determination Battlemaster's Determination	30,000 Honor Points Honor Points, 40 Alterac Valley Mark of Honor Alterac Valley Mark of Honor
Best Snap Threat	Bloodlust Brooch Bloodlust Brooch	Vendor: G'eras (41 Badges of Justice)
Best Hybrid Bear + Cat Value	Badge of Tenacity Badge of Tenacity	Drop: Leotheras the Blind (Serpentshrine Cavern)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:

Best Sustained Threat (above Hit cap)	Dragonspine Trophy Dragonspine Trophy	Drop: Gruul the Dragonkiller (Gruul's Lair)

Best in Slot Weapons for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 3
Pillar of Ferocity is the best PvE Feral weapon introduced in Phase 3, and should be your default weapon choice in any tanking scenario. However, Vengeful Gladiator's Staff can be considered as a Defense swap in more aggressive gear sets that are optimized for hybrid roles where you are splitting your time between tanking in Dire Bear Form and DPSing in Cat Form, allowing you to drop defensive pieces like Gilded Thorium Cloak Gilded Thorium Cloak and Violet Signet of the Great Protector Violet Signet of the Great Protector in situations where they are overkill while still maintaining crit immunity.
Rank	Item	Source

Alternative	Wildfury Greatstaff Wildfury Greatstaff	Drop: Serpentshrine Cavern Trash Mobs

Idols for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 3
When optimizing for overall raid value, Idol of the Raven Goddess from the Swift Flight Form quest chain remains the top Idol choice for any Feral Druid who is placed in a stacked physical DPS group. For personal TPS, however, Idol of Brutality and Idol of the Wild are finally replaced by the new Phase 3 option, Idol of the White Stag.
Rank	Item	Source

Alternative	Idol of the Wild Idol of the Wild	Quest: Colossal Menace Colossal Menace

NerdEgghead
Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2022/01/25 (Burning Crusade)
The stat weights presented in my stat priority guide were a bit out of date, I've just updated them to reflect our current assessment of Phase 3 weights. You'll see that 80 Attack Power is about 4 AP higher in value for TPS than 40 Critical Strike Rating under these weights, which is why Battlemaster's Determination is ranked above Battlemaster's Cruelty in this guide. However, the difference is extremely minor, so either trinket can be chosen based on personal preference.

By Ainzooalgoun (3,400 – 7·11) on 2022/02/09 (Burning Crusade)	
	
Hey NerdEgg, I wanted to run an idea by that I'm sure has been said before but for some reason can't find a clear answer to online. I wanted to ask if there's such a thing as a "consistency set" where a bear can put on Gloves of the Searing Grip , Belt of One-Hundred Deaths , and Earthwarden to reach 5.25% expertise (the highest we can reach in P3 as far as I can tell) and try to get parried or dodged as infrequently as possible to reach the highest number of successful pulls as possible, when pure threat generation is a non-issue as long as my hits land.
Last edited by Ainzooalgoun on 2022/02/09

2

	

NerdEgghead <Guide Author> on 2022/02/09 (Burning Crusade)
Assigning some value to pull variance mitigation is totally fine (especially when your average TPS is well above what is needed for your guild), but Gloves of the Searing Grip and Earthwarden are both extremely inefficient swaps for that goal. In both cases, your EHP drops significantly and your DTPS will go up significantly due to the very poor stat allocation on the Expertise pieces when compared to the T6 gloves and Pillar of Ferocity. Furthermore, even when wearing all three of those Expertise pieces, the probability that your opener fails will still be 10% or more, so making those inefficient swaps will not guarantee you any pull consistency. Whether or not you wear those pieces, your DPS absolutely will have to pay attention on pull and give you a global or two before going all-out. As a result, your raid's overall DPS will not actually change at all with or without those pieces equipped if your DPS are doing their job, since they'll be holding back on pull either way. A far, far better source of pull variance mitigation than the weapon and glove swaps is Super Sapper Charge Super Sapper Charge: you should be using one of these on your second global of every pull if your opener does not land successfully, and doing so will solve your pull variance problems almost all the time without the need for gear swaps. If you want to build in even more security on pull, then you can use Abacus of Violent Odds Abacus of Violent Odds, which produces the most burst Threat immediately on pull of any trinket in the game, and has a higher likelihood of saving a failed pull than any of the Expertise gear does.

Burning Crusade Classic Feral Druid Tank Best in Slot (BiS) Phase 4 Gear Guide
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/02/26
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Phase 4 Zul'Aman for Feral Druid Tank in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid Tank to acquire while they progress during the fourth phase of The Burning Crusade Classic, and contains gear sourced from Zul'Aman, Black Temple, and Mount Hyjal, plus other pieces from previous phases like Serpentshrine Cavern, Tempest Keep, heroic dungeons, arena PvP Gear, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.
Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 4
Note on terminology: This guide will often classify gear options as the best "Threat skewed" or the best "mit skewed" piece in a given slot. This does not necessarily mean that the piece has the highest possible TPS or max possible mitigation in that slot. Oftentimes, a gear piece with overall balanced Threat and mitigation stats, but which leans one way or another, is the ideal tanking choice. For example, several of the optimal Threat-skewed pieces will have slightly lower Threat stats than pure DPS gear, but with a lot more Stamina.

Note on gemming: In our Phase 1 and Phase 2 BiS guides, Stamina gems have generally been recommended as the default option in all slots except when gearing for beneficial socket bonuses or to meet the color requirements of the Relentless Earthstorm Diamond Relentless Earthstorm Diamond meta gem. In Phase 4, however, the overall avoidance levels on Feral gear get high enough that Agility gems come out on top as the most efficient default choice due to the increasing returns of avoidance towards survivability. Nevertheless, all of the available Epic quality tanking gems in Phase 4 are close enough in value that it is almost always worth gemming for socket bonuses using a mixture of Delicate Crimson Spinel Delicate Crimson Spinel, Shifting Shadowsong Amethyst Shifting Shadowsong Amethyst, and Glinting Pyrestone Glinting Pyrestone gems.

Note that, unlike all prior phases of Classic WoW (both vanilla and TBC), Wolfshead Helm Wolfshead Helm is no longer required for hybrid off-tank gear sets starting in Phase 3! This is because the 2-piece bonus of Thunderheart Harness opens up a new powershifting rotation in Cat Form that can work even without the extra 20 Energy per shift from Wolfshead.

Feral Druid Tanks looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Tank Gems & Enchants
Aldor vs. Scryer for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 4
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For bear tanks, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants for threat and avoidance, respectively. The Aldor threat enchant is Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance, and the Scryer version is Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, both of which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. Despite providing different distributions of Attack Power versus Critical Strike Rating, calculations show that both enchants provide nearly identical TPS value within current modeling accuracy. Technically, these enchants are not even used in a completely optimized, balanced gear set since the Naxxramas shoulder enchant is superior to both, but the vast majority of players will not have Sapphiron enchants saved up from Phase 6 of Classic.

Much later on in Phase 5, there is one tank item with a meaningfully different behavior for the two factions: Shattered Sun Pendant of Resolve Shattered Sun Pendant of Resolve. The Scryer version of the proc provides 100 Expertise Rating, which is substantially better than the Aldor version, which provides 100 Dodge Rating. Regardless of the proc, however, this neck is not close to best in slot for bears in Phase 5, and is outclassed by superior drops from the final raid, Sunwell Plateau. So feel free to choose either faction based on personal preference, as the choice will not impact your tanking performance whatsoever in the long run!

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Tank Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 4
When fully decked out in BiS gear, Cursed Vision of Sargeras barely edges out the Tier 6 helm for overall tanking value this phase. However, when considering your gear set as a whole, Vengeful Gladiator's Dragonhide Helm is the optimal choice for both defensively and offensively skewed gear sets, as the significant Resilience levels on the Arena helm allow you to accommodate the new Badge rewards Brooch of Deftness and Slikk's Cloak of Placation into your set while maintaining crit immunity.
Rank	Item	Source
Best Individually	Cursed Vision of Sargeras Cursed Vision of Sargeras	Drop: Illidan Stormrage (Black Temple)

Alternative	Thunderheart Cover Thunderheart Cover	Drop: Helm of the Forgotten Vanquisher - Archimonde (Hyjal Summit)
Alternative	Nordrassil Headdress Nordrassil Headdress	Drop: Helm of the Vanquished Defender - Lady Vashj (Serpentshrine Cavern)

Best in Slot Wrist Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 4
The epic PvP off-set bracers from Season 3 are the best tanking bracers for the entirety of Phase 3-4, just as the Season 1-2 bracers were for Phase 1-2. In Season 3, the "Dragonhide" version of the PvP off-set is given a complete stat overhaul, removing the +Healing stats on it in exchange for valuable Critical Strike Rating. As a result, Vindicator's Dragonhide Bracers Vindicator's Dragonhide Bracers also becomes the best in slot cat DPS bracers for Phase 3-4, making them especially important to acquire for Feral off-tanks. Note that Insidious Bands Insidious Bands are better bear tanking bracers individually, but the difference is small enough that the PvP bracers are the very first piece to swap in when building a crit immune gear set.

Best in Slot Neck Jewelry for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 4
The new Badge of Justice Badge of Justice reward Brooch of Deftness is by far the most significant tanking upgrade for Feral Druids in Phase 4, providing 100 AP equivalent of value increase relative to the Phase 3 necklace Pendant of Titans. The jump in value provided by the Badge neckpiece is large enough that it is no longer efficient to use the neck slot for crit immunity even in heavily mit skewed gear sets. Note that when optimizing for overall raid value rather than just personal tanking value, Braided Eternium Chain should be prioritized instead, as its on-use effect can provide as much as 100 DPS to your party, and you can easily live without the additional Stamina or Expertise on Brooch of Deftness when fully decked out in Tier 6 quality gear.

Best in Slot Ring Jewelry for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 4
Quite remarkably, Violet Signet of the Great Protector from Karazhan retains its place as the highest value tanking ring even in Phase 4, and should pretty much always be used when performing a main tank role in raids. In the second ring slot, Ring of Deceitful Intent is the optimal choice in isolation, but in practice, you may not have sufficient Hit budget available in your gear set to efficiently utilize it when raiding with a Balance Druid Druid providing the Improved Faerie Fire debuff. In this situation, Band of the Eternal Champion is the best alternative that lacks Hit Rating, providing an excellent balance of Stamina, avoidance, and DPS.

When building a more aggressive gear set for hybrid off-tanking + DPS roles, the Armor ring can optionally be dropped in favor of a second DPS ring. Once again, the optimal choice in this scenario depends on whether or not your raid uses the Improved Faerie Fire debuff: Stormrage Signet Ring is the top Threat/DPS ring for Feral off-tanks in 9% Hit setups, while [the others listed are the top ranking alternatives to Band of the Eternal Champion in 6% Hit setups.

Best in Slot Trinkets for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 4
Phase 3 of TBC introduced a new class of on-use health trinkets, which we will refer to as "Last Stand trinkets" due to their similarity to the Warrior Warrior  Last Stand ability. These trinkets serve as powerful defensive cooldowns that can easily save your life when used immediately after a burst damage spike from a raid boss or trash mob. Since bear TPS in Phase 4 gear will vastly exceed what is required to hold aggro against even very competitive DPS players, a skilled Feral tank can easily justify dropping the Threat trinkets that were used in Phase 1 and 2 (such as Bloodlust Brooch and Dragonspine Trophy) in order to equip one or two Last Stand trinkets as a safety net against rare near-death scenarios when tanking, which are the most likely cause of raid wipes in any competitive guild. The three best Last Stand trinket options in Phase 4 are, in order: Shadowmoon Insignia, Battlemaster's Determination, and Battlemaster's Cruelty. The two Battlemaster trinkets share a cooldown and cannot be used together, but do not share a cooldown with Shadowmoon Insignia. This means that when main tanking any hard-hitting raid boss, it is optimal to equip this trinket plus either one of the Battlemaster trinkets (the AP and Crit passives have near identical value for bears), allowing you to pop both trinkets together for a massive 3500 temporary HP boost when needed.

If you opt not to use a double Last Stand trinket pairing, then the best alternative options are the same as in Phase 2: Badge of Tenacity for mitigation, Bloodlust Brooch and Berserker's Call for burst Threat, and any of our previous recommended BiS threat trinkets for overall threat.
Rank	Item	Source
Best Defensive Cooldown	Shadowmoon Insignia Shadowmoon Insignia	Drop: Gurtogg Bloodboil (Black Temple)
Best Defensive Cooldown	Battlemaster's Determination Battlemaster's Determination	30,000 Honor Points Honor Points, 40 Alterac Valley Mark of Honor Alterac Valley Mark of Honor
Best Snap Threat	Abacus of Violent Odds Abacus of Violent Odds	Drop: Pathaleon the Calculator (The Mechanar)
Best Snap Threat	Berserker's Call Berserker's Call	Drop: Zul'jin - Zul'Aman

NerdEgghead
Wowhead Classic Feral Druid Writer
Nerdegghead is an avid Classic WoW fan and theorycrafter who wrote the Feral Druid class guides for Classic through Wrath of the Lich King. He mained a warlock during TBC and Wrath, and frequented the Elitist Jerks theorycrafting forums back then as a lurker. Nerdegghead creates sim tools and stat weight calculators for tanking and cat DPS, and serves a moderator on the druid class Discord where they collaborate on these tools and help out players of all skill levels.

Burning Crusade Classic Feral Druid Tank Best in Slot (BiS) Phase 5 Gear Guide
By NerdEgghead
Updated: 2026/02/25
Welcome to Wowhead's Best in Slot Gear list from Phase 5 Sunwell Plateau for Feral Druid Tank in The Burning Crusade Classic. This guide will list the recommended gear for Feral Druid Tank to acquire while they progress during the fifth and final phase of The Burning Crusade Classic, which contains gear sourced from Sunwell Plateau, Zul'Aman, Black Temple, and Mount Hyjal, plus other pieces from previous phases like Serpentshrine Cavern, Tempest Keep, heroic dungeons, arena PvP Gear, professions, BoE World Drops, and reputations.
Best In Slot Gear for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 5
Note on terminology: This guide will often classify gear options as the best "Threat skewed" or the best "mit skewed" piece in a given slot. This does not necessarily mean that the piece has the highest possible TPS or maximum possible mitigation in that slot. Oftentimes, a gear piece with overall balanced Threat and mitigation stats, but which leans one way or another, is the ideal tanking choice. For example, several of the optimal Threat-skewed pieces will have slightly lower Threat stats than pure DPS gear, but with a lot more Stamina.

Note on gemming: In our Phase 3 and Phase 4 BiS guides, Agility gems were recommended as the most efficient default choice due to the sky-high avoidance of Tier 6 geared bears and the increasing returns of avoidance towards survivability. For Phase 5 content, however, stacking Stamina gems once again becomes the optimal choice for survivability due to the Sunwell Radiance aura, which drops the baseline avoidance of Ferals back down to Phase 1 levels and nerfs the overall value of incremental avoidance gains from gems. Another difference in gemming recommendations relative to prior phases is the use of the Powerful Earthstorm Diamond Powerful Earthstorm Diamond meta gem in place of the previously recommended Relentless Earthstorm Diamond Relentless Earthstorm Diamond. This is a consequence of deprioritizing TPS relative to survivability for Phase 5 content due to the substantially higher risk of death when tanking Sunwell Plateau bosses compared to prior raid tiers, and also because the simpler color requirements on Powerful Earthstorm Diamond Powerful Earthstorm Diamond allow you to fit in at least 2 additional Solid Empyrean Sapphire Solid Empyrean Sapphire gems than would be possible when gemming to activate Relentless Earthstorm Diamond Relentless Earthstorm Diamond.

Feral Druid Tanks looking to add Enchants and Gems to their BiS gear should take a look at our guide below:

Feral Druid Tank Gems & Enchants
Aldor vs. Scryer for Feral Druid Tank in Phase 5
Unlike most other specs in TBC, the choice of Aldor versus Scryer faction has almost zero impact on the long-term PvE performance of a Feral Druid. For bear tanks, the only permanently relevant items provided by these factions are the two shoulder enchants for Threat and avoidance, respectively. The Aldor Threat enchant is Greater Inscription of Vengeance Greater Inscription of Vengeance and the Scryer version is Greater Inscription of the Blade Greater Inscription of the Blade, both of which require Exalted reputation with the Aldor and Scryer factions, respectively. Despite providing different distributions of Attack Power versus Critical Strike Rating, calculations show that both enchants provide nearly identical TPS value within current modeling accuracy. Technically, these enchants are not even used in an optimal threat-skewed gear set since Might of the Scourge Might of the Scourge is superior to both, but the vast majority of players will not have Sapphiron enchants saved up from Phase 6 of Classic.

For mitigation, the Aldor faction offers Greater Inscription of Warding Greater Inscription of Warding at Exalted reputation while the Scryers offer Greater Inscription of the Knight Greater Inscription of the Knight. Due to the way that Blizzard scaled rating stats in TBC, it turns out that 1 Defense Rating and 1 Dodge Rating provide an identical average reduction of DTPS (damage taken per second). So just like the DPS enchants, the two defensive enchant options provide the same average mitigation value, as they simply trade 5 Dodge Rating for 5 Defense Rating.

In Phase 5, there is one new tank item with a meaningfully different behavior for the two factions: Shattered Sun Pendant of Resolve Shattered Sun Pendant of Resolve. The Scryer version of the proc provides 100 Expertise Rating, which is substantially better than the Aldor version, which provides 100 Dodge Rating. Regardless of the proc, however, this neck is not close to best in slot for bears in Phase 5, and is outclassed by superior Sunwell Plateau drops like Clutch of Demise Clutch of Demise and Collar of the Pit Lord Collar of the Pit Lord. So feel free to choose either faction based on personal preference, as the choice will not impact your tanking performance whatsoever in the long run!

If you need to grind reputation with The Aldor or The Scryers, or any of the other factions that offer BiS gear for Feral Tank Druids, take a look at our guides to learn the fastest way to reach the rank you'll need.

Reputations Overview TBC Classic
Best in Slot Head Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
The new Sunwell off-piece helmet, Mask of the Fury Hunter, is a significant upgrade in both survivability and Threat relative to Phase 4 options.

Best in Slot Back Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
The new best-in-slot tanking cloak Crimson Paragon's Cover is phenomenally itemized with a mixture of very high Stamina and avoidance stats, combined with a high TPS/DPS contribution from Expertise Rating. Unlike all prior phases of TBC, cloaks with elevated Armor budget (such as Gilded Thorium Cloak and Slikk's Cloak of Placation) are no longer great alternatives once you have access to Sunwell Plateau loot, since both mit skewed and Threat skewed gear sets will already be Armor capped from other gear slots.

Best in Slot Chest Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
Harness of Carnal Instinct is by far the best tanking chest piece in Phase 5, with no competitive alternatives in the slot. Note that if you play an off-tank role in your raid team, you will likely be forced to choose between Bladed Chaos Tunic for DPS and Harness of Carnal Instinct for tanking, as the two items are sourced from the same raid drop. One gearing strategy worth considering is taking Harness of Carnal Instinct early on in the phase, when Ferals should tank almost every boss due to their much higher survivability relative to the other tank classes, and later trading it in for Bladed Chaos Tunic using a Sunmote Sunmote once the content is on farm and you can go back to DPSing most encounters.

Best in Slot Wrist Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
Phase 5 marks the first time in TBC that PvP offset bracers are not best in slot for bear tanks in their respective season, since Blizzard finally gave Feral tanks a raid-quality PvE bracer option via the new Sunwell Tier 6 wrist token. Not only do Thunderheart Wristguards outperform the Season 4 PvP options individually, but they also let you maintain the 2-piece Thunderheart Harness set bonus while accumulating other Sunwell Plateau off-piece upgrades.

Alternative	Band of the Swift Paw Band of the Swift Paw	Vendor: G'eras (35 Badges of Justice)
Best in Slot Hand Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
Similar to the other armor off-pieces from Sunwell, Gloves of the Forest Drifter significantly outclass all alternative Phase 5 options for bears.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS	Gloves of the Forest Drifter Gloves of the Forest Drifter	Vendor: Yrma - Shadowed Gauntlets of Paroxysm Shadowed Gauntlets of Paroxysm + 1 Sunmote Sunmote - M'uru (Sunwell Plateau)
Mit Skewed Alternative	Handwraps of the Aggressor Handwraps of the Aggressor	Vendor: Smith Hauthaa, (75 Badges of Justice)
Threat Skewed Alternative	Shadowed Gauntlets of Paroxysm Shadowed Gauntlets of Paroxysm	Drop: M'uru (Sunwell Plateau)
Mit Skewed Alternative	Thunderheart Gauntlets Thunderheart Gauntlets	Drop: Gloves of the Forgotten Vanquisher - Azgalor (Hyjal Summit)


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Hand Armor Recommendations


Rank	Item	Source
Threat Skewed Alternative	Gloves of Immortal Dusk Gloves of Immortal Dusk	Profession: Leatherworking (BoE)

Best in Slot Waist Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
Belt of One-Hundred Deaths is finally replaced in Phase 5 BiS sets by alternatives with better overall tanking value. In heavily mit-skewed sets, such as the first embedded gear set at the top of this guide, Guardian's Leather Belt is a better choice due to its higher avoidance plus the contribution of its Resilience Rating towards reaching crit immunity. In more aggressive threat-skewed sets, such as the second embedded gear set in this guide, Thunderheart Waistguard is a superior choice because it is required to unlock the 4-piece Thunderheart Harness set bonus once you obtain your non-tier best pieces. Keep in mind that pretty much any endgame tanking set will be nearly Expertise soft-capped, which means that the additional Expertise Rating on Belt of One-Hundred Deaths only provides half the value that it used to in prior phases.
Rank	Item	Source
Best Mit Skewed	Guardian's Leather Belt Guardian's Leather Belt	17,850 Honor Points Honor Points, 20 Arathi Basin Mark of Honor Arathi Basin Mark of Honor
Best Threat Skewed	Thunderheart Waistguard Thunderheart Waistguard	Drop: Belt of the Forgotten Vanquisher - Brutallus (Sunwell Plateau)]
Alternative	Belt of One-Hundred Deaths Belt of One-Hundred Deaths	Drop: Lady Vashj (Serpentshrine Cavern)
Alternative	Waistguard of the Great Beast Waistguard of the Great Beast	Vendor: G'eras (60 Badges of Justice)
Best in Slot Leg Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
Of all the off-piece armor upgrades you can obtain in Sunwell Plateau, Leggings of the Immortal Beast are the most impactful by a landslide, providing a whopping 62 Stamina increase (when gemmed) over the previously BiS Tier 6 legs, on top of higher Armor and better Threat stats as well. Similar to the chest slot, Ferals playing an off-tank role in their raid team will be forced to pick between Leggings of the Immortal Night for cat DPS and Leggings of the Immortal Beast for bear tanking, with the bear version of the leggings performing around 66 DPS worse in Cat Form. Unlike the analogous chest pieces, however, you will not be able to convert the tank legs into DPS ones at a later date once the content is on farm, so make sure to plan out your choice in advance based on your raid's tanking assignments.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS	Leggings of the Immortal Beast Leggings of the Immortal Beast	Vendor: Yrma - Leggings of the Immortal Night Leggings of the Immortal Night + 1 Sunmote Sunmote - Felmyst (Sunwell Plateau)
Mit Skewed Alternative	Tameless Breeches Tameless Breeches	Vendor: Smith Hauthaa (60 Badges of Justice)
Threat Skewed Alternative	Leggings of the Immortal Night Leggings of the Immortal Night	Drop: Felmyst (Sunwell Plateau)
Alternative	Thunderheart Leggings Thunderheart Leggings	Drop: Leggings of the Forgotten Vanquisher - The Illidari Council (Black Temple)
Best in Slot Feet Armor for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
Thunderheart Treads are the biggest upgrade of the three new Tier 6 pieces from Sunwell Plateau, and should be prioritized over the bracers and belt if you have to choose.
Rank	Item	Source
BiS	Thunderheart Treads Thunderheart Treads	Drop: Boots of the Forgotten Vanquisher - Felmyst (Sunwell Plateau)
Alternative	Treads of the Den Mother Treads of the Den Mother	Zone Drop - Black Temple

Best in Slot Neck Jewelry for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
Brutallus drops the new best-in-slot tanking necklace, Collar of the Pit Lord, which is optimal in both Threat-skewed and mit-skewed gear sets due to its combination of ultra-high Stamina plus a substantial amount of Expertise Rating (the highest value TPS/DPS stat by far when under the soft cap).
Rank	Item	Source
BiS	Collar of the Pit Lord Collar of the Pit Lord	Drop: Brutallus (Sunwell Plateau)
Mit Skewed Alternative	Pendant of Titans Pendant of Titans	Drop: Essence of Anger (Black Temple)

Threat Skewed Alternative	Hard Khorium Choker Hard Khorium Choker	Profession: Jewelcrafting (BoP)
Best in Slot Ring Jewelry for Feral Druid Tank in TBC Classic Phase 5
Phase 5 finally ends the long reign of Violet Signet of the Great Protector from Karazhan as the best bear tanking ring, due to the arrival of two even better Armor rings, Ring of Hardened Resolve and Ring of the Stalwart Protector. Despite the useless Shield Block Value on it, Ring of Hardened Resolve is so excellently itemized for bear tanks that it should be permanently equipped in any Phase 5 tanking set. The second ring slot is more flexible and depends on how you choose to itemize the rest of your gear set: Ring of the Stalwart Protector Ring of the Stalwart Protector is the optimal choice when under the Armor cap (35,880 raid buffed Armor), while Ring of the Stalwart Protector is optimal in sets that are already Armor capped (such as the mit skewed example set presented at the start of this guide).
Rank	Item	Source
Best Overall	Ring of Hardened Resolve Ring of Hardened Resolve	Drop: M'uru / Entropius (Sunwell Plateau)
Best Below Armor Cap	Ring of the Stalwart Protector Ring of the Stalwart Protector	Vendor: Smith Hauthaa - (60 Badges of Justice)
Best Above Armor Cap	Hard Khorium Band Hard Khorium Band	Profession: Jewelcrafting (BoP)
Alternative (below Armor cap)	Violet Signet of the Great Protector Violet Signet of the Great Protector	Quest:Path of the Violet Protector Path of the Violet Protector - Requires Exalted with The Violet Eye


If you don't have access to the recommendations above, here are further options to choose from or farm:
Other Ring Jewelry Recommendations
